📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: bioRxiv (Cold Spring Harbor Laboratory)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-25  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.08.18.745466  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

神经氨酸酶和聚合酶活性受损与人类呼吸道培养物中 B3.13 和 D1.1 H5N1 谱系的气溶胶感染性有限相对应

IMPAIRED NEURAMINIDASE AND POLYMERASE ACTIVITIES CORRESPOND WITH LIMITED AEROSOL INFECTIVITY OF B3.13 AND D1.1 H5N1 LINEAGES IN HUMAN RESPIRATORY CULTURES
作者： L. Claire Gay, Dikshya Regmi, Flavio Cargnin Faccin, Ignacio Scanarotti, Alison Mark, C. Joaquín Cáceres, Teresa Mejias, Maria Corkran, Margaret A. Scull, Rafael A. Medina
摘要中文翻译摘要 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒的持续流行已达到临界点,其特点是史无前例的哺乳动物 跨物种溢出 和美国持续的 暴发。虽然这些病毒在传统雪貂模型中仍然具有高度致命性,但人类感染(主要与 B3.13 和 D1.1 谱系有关)明显较轻,通常表现为结膜炎,很少累及呼吸道。在这项研究中,我们通过使用包含气溶胶沉降室的生理相关气液界面 (ALI) 培养系统评估最近 H5N1 分离株的感染性来解决这种脱节问题。我们证明,虽然直接液体接种可实现有效复制,但雾化 H5N1 菌株在感染人类呼吸道上皮的能力方面表现出显着缺陷。相比之下,无论接种途径如何,原型 H5N1 病毒在雪貂中仍然具有高致病性和致命性,显示出系统性传播到大脑和其他器官。我们的研究结果确定了导致这种呼吸限制的两个主要病毒决定因素:神经氨酸酶(NA)酶活性降低和聚合酶活性受损。总的来说,这些结果表明常用的哺乳动物模型可能夸大了当前人类大流行的风险。这项工作强调了对替代风险评估平台的迫切需要,以确定这些病毒所需的特定基因转变,以克服人类适应的现有障碍。重要性 当前的大流行风险评估在很大程度上依赖动物模型,特别是雪貂,来评估新出现的流感病毒造成的威胁。然而,最近主要引起轻度人类感染的 H5N1 病毒在这些模型中仍然具有高毒力,这给它们预测人类疾病的能力带来了不确定性。使用通过气溶胶暴露感染的生理相关人类气道模型,我们证明,尽管在雪貂中保留了高毒力,但当代 H5N1 建立呼吸道感染的能力明显受到限制。这些发现强调了将人类气道气溶胶模型纳入大流行风险评估框架的重要性,以更好地评估人类适应和新兴流感病毒的呼吸道感染潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决动物模型中近期 H5N1 病毒的高毒力与观察到的轻度人类感染之间的脱节,特别是 B3.13 和 D1.1 谱系。该研究旨在评估这些 H5N1 分离株在人类呼吸道培养物中的感染性。
设计与对象
本研究使用具有气溶胶沉降室的生理相关气液界面 (ALI) 培养系统来评估近期 H5N1 分离株的感染性。该研究是在实验室环境中进行的,重点关注人类呼吸道上皮细胞。
核心方法
研究人员使用通过气溶胶暴露感染的人体气道模型评估了最近 H5N1 分离株的传染性。他们采用了气溶胶沉降室来模拟现实条件并评估病毒复制和传播。
主要结果
研究表明,尽管通过直接液体接种进行有效复制,雾化的 H5N1 菌株在感染人类呼吸道上皮的能力方面表现出显着缺陷。相比之下,无论接种途径如何,原型 H5N1 病毒在雪貂中仍然具有高致病性和致命性。
解释与创新
结果表明,在当代 H5N1 病毒中观察到的呼吸限制是由于神经氨酸酶 (NA) 酶活性降低和聚合酶活性受损所致,这是关键的病毒决定因素。这些发现凸显了当前大流行风险评估中的关键差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了将人类气道气溶胶模型纳入大流行风险评估框架的重要性,以更好地评估人类适应和新兴流感病毒的呼吸道感染潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对单一人类气道模型的依赖而受到限制,并且没有考虑其他潜在的 宿主 因素。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它直接比较了生理相关系统中的气雾化和液体接种方法。

IMPAIRED NEURAMINIDASE AND POLYMERASE ACTIVITIES CORRESPOND WITH LIMITED AEROSOL INFECTIVITY OF B3.13 AND D1.1 H5N1 LINEAGES IN HUMAN RESPIRATORY CULTURES

Authors: L. Claire Gay, Dikshya Regmi, Flavio Cargnin Faccin, Ignacio Scanarotti, Alison Mark, C. Joaquín Cáceres, Teresa Mejias, Maria Corkran, Margaret A. Scull, Rafael A. Medina
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The ongoing panzootic of clade 2.3.4.4b highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 viruses has reached a critical point, marked by unprecedented mammalian spillover and sustained outbreaks in U.S. While these viruses remain highly lethal in traditional ferret models, human infections—primarily linked to the B3.13 and D1.1 lineages—have been notably mild, typically presenting as conjunctivitis with minimal respiratory involvement. In this study, we address this disconnect by evaluating the infectivity of recent H5N1 isolates using a physiologically relevant air-liquid interface (ALI) culture system that incorporates an aerosol settling chamber. We demonstrate that while direct liquid inoculation leads to efficient replication, aerosolized H5N1 strains exhibit a significant defect in their ability to infect human respiratory epithelium. In contrast, a prototypic H5N1 virus remains highly pathogenic and lethal in ferrets regardless of the inoculation route, showing systemic dissemination to the brain and other organs. Our findings identify two primary viral determinants driving this respiratory restriction: reduced neuraminidase (NA) enzymatic activity and impaired polymerase activity. Collectively, these results suggest that commonly used mammalian models may overstate current human pandemic risk. This work highlights the critical need for alternative risk- assessment platforms to identify the specific genetic shifts required for these viruses to overcome existing barriers to human adaptation. IMPORTANCE Current pandemic risk assessments rely heavily on animal models, particularly ferrets, to evaluate the threat posed by emerging influenza viruses. However, recent H5N1 viruses that have caused predominantly mild human infections have remained highly virulent in these models, creating uncertainty about how well they predict human disease. Using a physiologically relevant human airway model infected through aerosol exposure, we demonstrate that contemporary H5N1 are markedly restricted in their ability to establish respiratory infection despite retaining high virulence in ferrets. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating human airway aerosol models into pandemic risk assessment frameworks to better evaluate the human adaptation and respiratory infection potential of emerging influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the disconnect between the high virulence of recent H5N1 viruses in animal models and the mild human infections observed, particularly with the B3.13 and D1.1 lineages. The research aimed to evaluate the infectivity of these H5N1 isolates in human respiratory cultures.
Design and population
This study used a physiologically relevant air-liquid interface (ALI) culture system with an aerosol settling chamber to assess the infectivity of recent H5N1 isolates. The study was conducted in a laboratory setting and focused on human respiratory epithelium.
Core methods
The researchers evaluated the infectivity of recent H5N1 isolates using a human airway model infected through aerosol exposure. They incorporated an aerosol settling chamber to simulate realistic conditions and assessed viral replication and dissemination.
Main results
The study demonstrated that aerosolized H5N1 strains exhibit a significant defect in their ability to infect human respiratory epithelium, despite efficient replication through direct liquid inoculation. In contrast, a prototypic H5N1 virus remains highly pathogenic and lethal in ferrets regardless of the inoculation route.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that the respiratory restriction observed in contemporary H5N1 viruses is due to reduced neuraminidase (NA) enzymatic activity and impaired polymerase activity, which are key viral determinants. These findings highlight a critical gap in current pandemic risk assessments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of incorporating human airway aerosol models into pandemic risk assessment frameworks to better evaluate the human adaptation and respiratory infection potential of emerging influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on a single human airway model and does not account for other potential host factors. However, the evidence is strong as it directly compares aerosolized and liquid inoculation methods in a physiologically relevant system.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

缺氧诱导因子 1-α 抑制可减轻 H9N2 禽流感 病毒诱导的炎症反应。

Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha inhibition mitigates inflammatory responses induced by H9N2 avian influenza virus.
作者： Su-Ying Zhu, Bei-Bei Li, Ya-Qi Wang, Yu-Han Tian, Jia-Yue Zhang, Hong-Shan Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要 H9N2 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对家禽和公共卫生构成重大威胁,并有可能导致毒性更强的流感病毒株的出现。缺氧诱导因子 1-α (HIF-1α) 在病毒 发病机制、免疫调节和炎症中发挥着至关重要的作用,使其成为治疗干预的有希望的靶点。在这里,我们探讨了 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 AIV 发病机制 中的参与以及相关的炎症反应。我们的数据显示,HIF-1α 通过调节线粒体健康度,加剧了 H9N2 AIV 感染引起的炎症。此外,我们还研究了 Sotetsuflavone (Seo)(一种天然衍生的黄酮类化合物),了解其调节 H9N2 感染后炎症反应的潜力。分子对接结合表面等离子共振测定表明 Seo 对 HIF-1α 表现出强大的结合亲和力,这意味着直接抑制机制。随后的体外研究表明,Seo 通过抑制感染 H9N2 AIV 的 HD11 细胞中的 HIF-1α 信号通路,大大减少了炎症细胞因子的产生,包括 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α。相应地,体内实验证实,Seo 给药显着改善 H9N2 感染小鼠的肺部炎症,同时还增强肺泡上皮修复,II 型肺泡上皮细胞标记物 Abca3 和 Sftpb 表达增加证明了这一点。总的来说,这些发现将 Seo 确定为 HIF-1α 的有效抑制剂,突显了其在减轻与流感病毒感染相关的炎症和肺损伤方面的治疗潜力。重要性 H9N2 禽流感 病毒通过缺氧诱导因子 1 α (HIF-1α) 介导的线粒体功能失调引发炎症性肺损伤。分子对接和表面等离子共振测定证实,天然类黄酮 Sotetsuflavone (Seo) 直接结合并抑制 HIF-1α。在 H9N2 感染的 HD11 细胞中,Seo 抑制 HIF-1α 信号传导,减少 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α 的产生。在体内,Seo 治疗 可减轻小鼠肺部炎症并增强肺泡上皮修复,上调 II 型肺细胞标记物(Abca3 和 Sftpb)。这些发现表明 Seo 是一种有前途的 HIF-1α 靶向疗法,用于治疗流感相关的免疫病理学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
H9N2 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 对家禽和公共卫生构成重大威胁,并有可能导致毒性更强的流感病毒株的出现。缺氧诱导因子 1-α (HIF-1α) 在病毒 发病机制、免疫调节和炎症中发挥着至关重要的作用,使其成为治疗干预的有希望的靶点。
设计与对象
该研究探讨了 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 AIV 发病机制 中的参与以及相关的炎症反应。使用感染H9N2 AIV的HD11细胞进行体外实验,并在感染H9N2的小鼠中进行体内实验以评估肺部炎症和肺泡上皮修复。
核心方法
分子对接结合表面等离子共振测定用于评估 Sotetsuflavone (Seo) 与 HIF-1α 的结合亲和力。体外研究评估了 Seo 通过抑制 H9N2 感染的 HD11 细胞中的 HIF-1α 信号通路对炎症细胞因子产生(IL-1β、IL-6、TNF-α)的影响。
主要结果
Seo 通过抑制 HIF-1α 信号通路,大大减少了 H9N2 感染的 HD11 细胞中炎症细胞因子 IL-1β、IL-6 和 TNF-α 的产生。在体内,Seo 给药显着改善了 H9N2 感染小鼠的肺部炎症,并增强了肺泡上皮修复,II 型肺泡上皮细胞标记物 Abca3 和 Sftpb 表达增加证明了这一点。
解释与创新
研究结果表明 Seo 是 HIF-1α 的有效抑制剂,突显了其在减轻与 H9N2 流感病毒感染相关的炎症和肺损伤方面的治疗潜力。这代表了一种针对 禽流感 中 HIF-1α 介导的免疫病理学的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,Sotetsuflavone 可开发为一种治疗剂,减少 H9N2 禽流感 感染中的炎症性肺损伤,有可能改善临床结果并为未来的 抗病毒药物 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖体外 HD11 细胞模型和体内小鼠模型而受到限制,可能无法完全重现人类疾病的复杂性。虽然证据支持 HIF-1α 在 H9N2 发病机制 中的机制作用,但需要在人体系统和临床试验中进一步验证以确认治疗效果和安全性。

Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha inhibition mitigates inflammatory responses induced by H9N2 avian influenza virus.

Authors: Su-Ying Zhu, Bei-Bei Li, Ya-Qi Wang, Yu-Han Tian, Jia-Yue Zhang, Hong-Shan Wang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) poses significant threats to poultry and public health, with the potential to contribute to the emergence of more virulent influenza strains. Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha (HIF-1α) plays a crucial role in viral pathogenesis, immune regulation, and inflammation, making it a promising target for therapeutic intervention. Here, we explored the involvement of HIF-1α in H9N2 AIV pathogenesis and associated inflammatory responses. Our data revealed that HIF-1α exacerbates inflammation induced by H9N2 AIV infection through its regulation of mitochondrial fitness. Additionally, we investigated Sotetsuflavone (Seo), a naturally derived flavonoid, for its potential to modulate inflammatory responses following H9N2 infection. Molecular docking coupled with surface plasmon resonance assays indicated that Seo exhibits robust binding affinity to HIF-1α, implying a direct inhibitory mechanism. Subsequent in vitro studies demonstrated that Seo greatly reduced the production of inflammatory cytokines, including IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α, through suppressing the HIF-1α signaling pathway in HD11 cells infected with H9N2 AIV. Correspondingly, in vivo experiments established that Seo administration significantly ameliorated pulmonary inflammation in H9N2-infected mice, while also enhancing alveolar epithelial repair, as evidenced by increased expression of type II alveolar epithelial cell markers Abca3 and Sftpb. Collectively, these findings identify Seo as a potent inhibitor of HIF-1α, highlighting its therapeutic potential in mitigating inflammation and lung damage associated with influenza virus infection. IMPORTANCE H9N2 avian influenza virus triggers inflammatory lung injury via hypoxia-inducible factor-1 alpha (HIF-1α)-mediated dysregulation of mitochondrial function. The natural flavonoid Sotetsuflavone (Seo) directly binds and inhibits HIF-1α, as confirmed by molecular docking and surface plasmon resonance assays. In H9N2-infected HD11 cells, Seo suppresses HIF-1α signaling, reducing IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α production. In vivo , Seo treatment attenuates pulmonary inflammation and enhances alveolar epithelial repair in mice, upregulating type II pneumocyte markers (Abca3 and Sftpb). These findings identify Seo as a promising HIF-1α-targeted therapeutic for influenza-associated immunopathology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
H9N2 avian influenza virus (AIV) poses significant threats to poultry and public health, with the potential to contribute to the emergence of more virulent influenza strains. Hypoxia-inducible factor 1-alpha (HIF-1α) plays a crucial role in viral pathogenesis, immune regulation, and inflammation, making it a promising target for therapeutic intervention.
Design and population
The study explored the involvement of HIF-1α in H9N2 AIV pathogenesis and associated inflammatory responses. In vitro experiments were conducted using HD11 cells infected with H9N2 AIV, and in vivo experiments were performed in H9N2-infected mice to assess pulmonary inflammation and alveolar epithelial repair.
Core methods
Molecular docking coupled with surface plasmon resonance assays were used to evaluate the binding affinity of Sotetsuflavone (Seo) to HIF-1α. In vitro studies assessed the effect of Seo on inflammatory cytokine production (IL-1β, IL-6, TNF-α) via suppression of the HIF-1α signaling pathway in H9N2-infected HD11 cells.
Main results
Seo greatly reduced the production of inflammatory cytokines IL-1β, IL-6, and TNF-α in H9N2-infected HD11 cells through suppressing the HIF-1α signaling pathway. In vivo, Seo administration significantly ameliorated pulmonary inflammation in H9N2-infected mice and enhanced alveolar epithelial repair, as evidenced by increased expression of type II alveolar epithelial cell markers Abca3 and Sftpb.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify Seo as a potent inhibitor of HIF-1α, highlighting its therapeutic potential in mitigating inflammation and lung damage associated with H9N2 influenza virus infection. This represents a novel approach to targeting HIF-1α-mediated immunopathology in avian influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that Sotetsuflavone could be developed as a therapeutic agent to reduce inflammatory lung injury in H9N2 avian influenza infections, potentially improving clinical outcomes and informing future antiviral strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro HD11 cell models and in vivo mouse models, which may not fully recapture human disease complexity. While the evidence supports a mechanistic role for HIF-1α in H9N2 pathogenesis, further validation in human systems and clinical trials is required to confirm therapeutic efficacy and safety.

使用随机机制模型预测高致病性 禽流感:澳大利亚的表现和教训

Forecasting high pathogenicity avian influenza with a stochastic mechanistic model: performance and lessons for Australia
作者： Meryl Theng, Simin Lee, Michelle Wille, Thao P. Le, Andrew C. Breed, Carlos Donoghue, Christopher M. Baker, Simon M. Firestone
摘要中文翻译高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 引起了全球性的大流行,对野生动物和牲畜造成了前所未有的影响,因此基于证据的疾病缓解和 暴发 应对至关重要。在本文中,我们描述了为 HPAI 建模挑战赛开发的传染病动力学时空机制模型及其对澳大利亚预测和政策的影响。为了模拟紧急响应条件,我们采用了现有模型来进行快速部署,而不是开发定制模型。我们在挑战中迭代地改进了模型,以更好地分析提供的 暴发 数据。在整个挑战过程中,我们准确地预测了时间趋势和局部 暴发 传播,但无法预测更罕见的长距离传播事件。该挑战在澳大利亚首次检测到 HPAI H5N1 之前(2026 年 6 月)就结束了,这为测试我们的响应模型是否准备好应对野生动物入侵和潜在的 跨物种溢出 商业家禽入侵提供了一个关键的机会。我们的经验确定了澳大利亚 HPAI H5N1 防备的三个关键考虑因素: 有针对性地增强我们的模型,以提高预测精度并实现基于情景的政策评估;现有建模基础设施对于快速应急响应的关键价值;研究和政策机构之间的持续合作,使建模能力与 暴发 响应要求保持一致。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 引起了全球性的大流行,对野生动物和牲畜造成了前所未有的影响,因此基于证据的疾病缓解和 暴发 应对至关重要。
设计与对象
该挑战在澳大利亚首次检测到 HPAI H5N1 之前(2026 年 6 月)就结束了,这为测试我们的响应模型是否准备好应对野生动物入侵和潜在的 跨物种溢出 商业家禽入侵提供了一个关键的机会。
核心方法
为了模拟紧急响应条件,我们采用了现有模型来进行快速部署,而不是开发定制模型。我们在挑战中迭代地改进了模型,以更好地分析提供的 暴发 数据。
主要结果
在整个挑战过程中,我们准确地预测了时间趋势和局部 暴发 传播,但无法预测更罕见的长距离传播事件。
解释与创新
我们的经验确定了澳大利亚 HPAI H5N1 防备的三个关键考虑因素: 有针对性地增强我们的模型,以提高预测精度并实现基于情景的政策评估;现有建模基础设施对于快速应急响应的关键价值;研究和政策机构之间的持续合作,使建模能力与 暴发 响应要求保持一致。
科研与公卫意义
这项挑战提供了一个重要的机会来测试我们对野生动物入侵和潜在的 跨物种溢出 商业家禽入侵的响应模型准备情况。
局限与证据强度
该挑战在澳大利亚首次检测到 HPAI H5N1 之前就结束了,限制了测试模型对现实世界 暴发 的预测能力的能力。

Forecasting high pathogenicity avian influenza with a stochastic mechanistic model: performance and lessons for Australia

Authors: Meryl Theng, Simin Lee, Michelle Wille, Thao P. Le, Andrew C. Breed, Carlos Donoghue, Christopher M. Baker, Simon M. Firestone
Original abstract (English)
High pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has caused a global panzootic with unprecedented impacts on wildlife and livestock, making evidence-based disease mitigation and outbreak response critical. In this paper, we describe a spatiotemporal mechanistic model of infectious disease dynamics developed for the HPAI Modelling Challenge and its implications for forecasting and policy in Australia. To emulate emergency response conditions, we adapted an existing model for rapid deployment rather than developing a bespoke model. We refined the model iteratively across the challenge to better analyse the provided outbreak data. Throughout the challenge, we accurately forecast temporal trends and local outbreak spread, but could not predict rarer, long-distance dispersal events. The challenge ended before HPAI H5N1 was first detected in Australia (June 2026), providing a critical opportunity to test our response modelling readiness for an incursion in wildlife and potential spillover into commercial poultry. Our experience identifies three key considerations for Australia's HPAI H5N1 preparedness: targeted enhancements to our model to improve forecast precision and enable scenario-based policy evaluation; the critical value of pre-existing modelling infrastructure for rapid emergency response; and sustained collaboration between research and policy institutions to align modelling capabilities with outbreak response requirements.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b has caused a global panzootic with unprecedented impacts on wildlife and livestock, making evidence-based disease mitigation and outbreak response critical.
Design and population
The challenge ended before HPAI H5N1 was first detected in Australia (June 2026), providing a critical opportunity to test our response modelling readiness for an incursion in wildlife and potential spillover into commercial poultry.
Core methods
To emulate emergency response conditions, we adapted an existing model for rapid deployment rather than developing a bespoke model. We refined the model iteratively across the challenge to better analyse the provided outbreak data.
Main results
Throughout the challenge, we accurately forecast temporal trends and local outbreak spread, but could not predict rarer, long-distance dispersal events.
Interpretation and novelty
Our experience identifies three key considerations for Australia's HPAI H5N1 preparedness: targeted enhancements to our model to improve forecast precision and enable scenario-based policy evaluation; the critical value of pre-existing modelling infrastructure for rapid emergency response; and sustained collaboration between research and policy institutions to align modelling capabilities with outbreak response requirements.
Scientific and public-health significance
The challenge provided a critical opportunity to test our response modelling readiness for an incursion in wildlife and potential spillover into commercial poultry.
Limitations and evidence strength
The challenge ended before HPAI H5N1 was first detected in Australia, limiting the ability to test the model's predictive capacity for real-world outbreaks.

2025 年,没有证据表明日本马感染 H5N1 禽流感 病毒。

No evidence of H5N1 avian influenza virus infection in Japanese horses in 2025.
作者： Nanako Kawanishi, Takahiro Hiono, Yoshinori Kambayashi, Hiroshi Bannai, Koji Tsujimura, Takashi Yamanaka, Norikazu Isoda, Yoshihiro Sakoda, Manabu Nemoto
摘要中文翻译进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5N1 高致病性禽流感 病毒的全球 跨物种溢出 进入哺乳动物 宿主 已成为公共卫生问题以及马产业的问题。然而,之前关于 H5N1 自然感染和马体内存在特异性抗体的报道仍然有限。因此,我们进行了一项血清监测研究,以评估从日本 400 匹马中收集的 H5N1 抗体的 患病率。分别使用针对 H5 重组蛋白和 H5N1 病毒抗原的竞争性 ELISA (cELISA) 和血凝抑制 (HI) 测试对样品进行筛选。在 400 个样本中,cELISA 检测结果显示 399 个样本(99.75%)呈阴性,而 1 个样本(0.25%)的结果可疑。然而,所有样本均显示 HI 滴度<8,证实不存在 H5N1 抗体。这些发现表明,目前日本马匹感染 H5N1 的风险仍然有限。尽管如此,有必要进行持续的全球 监测 监测 H5N1 跨物种溢出 对马种群的潜在风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决由于进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5N1 高致病性禽流感 病毒在全球 跨物种溢出 进入哺乳动物 宿主 造成的日益严重的公共卫生问题和对马业的影响。
设计与对象
日本于 2025 年至 2026 年进行了一项血清监测研究,涉及从不同地点收集的 400 匹马。
核心方法
该研究使用竞争性 ELISA (cELISA) 和血凝抑制 (HI) 测试分别针对 H5 重组蛋白和 H5N1 病毒抗原筛选样本。
主要结果
在 400 个样本中,cELISA 检测结果显示 399 个样本(99.75%)呈阴性,其中 1 个样本(0.25%)产生可疑结果。所有样本均显示 HI 效价<8,证实不存在 H5N1 抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,目前日本马匹感染 H5N1 的风险仍然有限,这为马匹群体中 H5N1 的状况提供了新的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解马群体中的 H5N1 禽流感 病毒,支持 监测 的工作并为公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于血清监测,可能无法捕获所有潜在的感染,并且由于样本量有限且存在可疑结果,证据不充分。

No evidence of H5N1 avian influenza virus infection in Japanese horses in 2025.

Authors: Nanako Kawanishi, Takahiro Hiono, Yoshinori Kambayashi, Hiroshi Bannai, Koji Tsujimura, Takashi Yamanaka, Norikazu Isoda, Yoshihiro Sakoda, Manabu Nemoto
Original abstract (English)
The global spillover of clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus into mammalian hosts has become a public health concern as well as to the equine industry. However, previous reports on H5N1 natural infection and the presence of specific antibodies in horses remain limited. Therefore, we conducted a serosurveillance study to assess the prevalence of H5N1 antibodies collected from 400 horses in Japan. Samples were screened using competitive ELISA (cELISA) and hemagglutination inhibition (HI) tests against H5-recombinant protein and H5N1 viral antigen, respectively. Of the 400 samples, 399 (99.75%) were negative by cELISA, while one sample (0.25%) yielded a doubtful result. However, all samples showed HI titers of <8, confirming the absence of H5N1 antibodies. These findings indicate that the current risk of H5N1 infection among horses in Japan remains limited. Nonetheless, continuous global surveillance is warranted to monitor the potential risk of H5N1 spillover into equine populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing public health concern and impact on the equine industry due to the global spillover of clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus into mammalian hosts.
Design and population
A serosurveillance study was conducted in Japan from 2025 to 2026, involving 400 horses collected across various locations.
Core methods
The study used competitive ELISA (cELISA) and hemagglutination inhibition (HI) tests to screen samples against H5-recombinant protein and H5N1 viral antigen, respectively.
Main results
Of the 400 samples, 399 (99.75%) were negative by cELISA, with one sample (0.25%) yielding a doubtful result. All samples showed HI titers of <8, confirming the absence of H5N1 antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the current risk of H5N1 infection among horses in Japan remains limited, providing novel evidence on the status of H5N1 in equine populations.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results contribute to the understanding of H5N1 avian influenza virus in equine populations, supporting surveillance efforts and informing public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on serosurveillance, which may not capture all potential infections, and the evidence is moderate due to the limited sample size and the presence of a doubtful result.

了解大流行应对中的道德权衡:禽流感 政策的离散选择实验。

Understanding moral trade-offs in pandemic response: A discrete choice experiment on avian influenza policies.
作者： Nicholas V. R. Smeele, Charlotte N. M. Waltz, Anja J. M. Schreijer, Pearl A. Dykstra
摘要中文翻译摘要 流行病防范需要在健康、经济和社会影响之间进行权衡;往往涉及道德紧张的选择。本研究调查了荷兰公众如何在 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 的潜在 暴发 背景下评估此类权衡。我们对 LISS 小组中 1,045 名受访者的代表性样本进行了离散选择实验 (DCE),研究了六种政策结果的偏好:死亡率、持久的身体和精神伤害、推迟医疗保健、社会限制和一次性纳税。潜在类别逻辑模型确定了四种不同的偏好概况,揭示了道德和经济推理中的巨大异质性。当生命或人类尊严等价值与金钱成本进行交易时,两个阶层表现出禁忌权衡厌恶(TTOA)。显示 TTOA 的受访者强调团结和政府责任,而对成本敏感的群体则优先考虑经济效率。这些发现强调,公众对流行病政策的接受程度不仅取决于有效性和成本,还取决于感知的道德合法性。在经济评估中考虑 TTOA 可以提高未来大​​流行防范策略的伦理考虑和社会可接受性。

问题与背景
流行病防范需要在健康、经济和社会影响之间进行权衡;往往涉及道德紧张的选择。
设计与对象
本研究调查了荷兰公众如何在 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 的潜在 暴发 背景下评估此类权衡。
核心方法
通过对 LISS 小组中 1,045 名受访者的代表性样本进行离散选择实验 (DCE),我们检查了六种政策结果的偏好:死亡率、持久的身体和精神伤害、推迟医疗保健、社会限制和一次性纳税。
主要结果
潜在类别逻辑模型确定了四种不同的偏好概况,揭示了道德和经济推理中的巨大异质性。
解释与创新
当生命或人类尊严等价值与金钱成本进行交易时,两个阶层表现出禁忌权衡厌恶(TTOA)。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
这些发现强调,公众对流行病政策的接受程度不仅取决于有效性和成本,还取决于感知的道德合法性。

Understanding moral trade-offs in pandemic response: A discrete choice experiment on avian influenza policies.

Authors: Nicholas V. R. Smeele, Charlotte N. M. Waltz, Anja J. M. Schreijer, Pearl A. Dykstra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Pandemic preparedness requires navigating trade-offs between health, economic, and social impacts; choices that often involve moral tensions. This study investigates how the Dutch public evaluates such trade-offs in the context of a potential outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI). Using a discrete choice experiment (DCE) administered to a representative sample of 1,045 respondents from the LISS panel, we examined preferences across six policy outcomes: mortality, lasting physical and mental injuries, postponed healthcare, social restrictions, and one-off tax payments. A latent class logit model identified four distinct preference profiles, revealing substantial heterogeneity in moral and economic reasoning. Two classes exhibited taboo trade-off aversion (TTOA) when values such as life or human dignity were traded against monetary costs. Respondents displaying TTOA emphasized solidarity and governmental responsibility, while cost-sensitive groups prioritized economic efficiency. These findings highlight that public acceptance of pandemic policies depends not only on effectiveness and cost but also on perceived moral legitimacy. Accounting for TTOA in economic evaluation can improve the ethical considerations and societal acceptability of future pandemic preparedness strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pandemic preparedness requires navigating trade-offs between health, economic, and social impacts; choices that often involve moral tensions.
Design and population
This study investigates how the Dutch public evaluates such trade-offs in the context of a potential outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI).
Core methods
Using a discrete choice experiment (DCE) administered to a representative sample of 1,045 respondents from the LISS panel, we examined preferences across six policy outcomes: mortality, lasting physical and mental injuries, postponed healthcare, social restrictions, and one-off tax payments.
Main results
A latent class logit model identified four distinct preference profiles, revealing substantial heterogeneity in moral and economic reasoning.
Interpretation and novelty
Two classes exhibited taboo trade-off aversion (TTOA) when values such as life or human dignity were traded against monetary costs.
Scientific and public-health significance
Respondents displaying TTOA emphasized solidarity and governmental responsibility, while cost-sensitive groups prioritized economic efficiency.
Limitations and evidence strength
These findings highlight that public acceptance of pandemic policies depends not only on effectiveness and cost but also on perceived moral legitimacy.

使用病毒 基因组 测序数据预测 禽流感 人兽共患病 的风险

Predicting the Risk of Avian Influenza Zoonosis using Viral Genome Sequencing Data
作者： Anna G Fairweather, Austin Andrews, Joshua Grier, Liam Brierley, Lorenzo Cattarino, Panovsk-Griffiths J, Jasmina Panovska‐Griffiths, Jasmina Panovska-Griffiths
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 感染的 宿主 范围广泛,尽管在野生水禽中具有天然的 储存宿主。虽然大多数菌株保留在其 宿主 物种内,但有些菌株通过遗传适应打破了物种障碍。我们最担心的是人畜共患病,即人类被感染的情况。尽管这些事件很少见,但它们与高 死亡率 相关,并引入了 AIV 人际传播 死亡率 的风险。作为人类中的一种新型病原体,它可能具有大流行的潜力。利用从病毒序列数据中提取的 8 种 AIV 蛋白的遗传组成特征,我们采用机器学习算法将 AIV 病例分类为人畜共患病或非人畜共患病。这些遗传特征编码 宿主“签名”,可以指示 人兽共患病 并包括频率测量,例如二肽组成和氨基酸理化特性。我们始终发现 XGBoost 优于所有其他算法。我们优化了十个分类模型的参数:一个用于 8 种蛋白质中的每一个,两个组合模型。接下来,我们表明多模型方法可以为 AIV 人兽共患病 提供最佳性能的预测。我们已鉴定出所有 8 种蛋白质在预测人畜共患病 传播 中发挥作用。特别重要的是 PB2 和 HA 蛋白,它们具有特定的氨基酸理化特性,例如电荷、二级结构和疏水性,是我们组合模型中最具指示性的特征。我们的免对齐计算研究可以识别仍然存在于禽类 宿主 中的 AIV 病例,这些病例在基因上最接近人畜共患 AIV 病例,从而识别出最有可能跨越物种障碍的病例。在资源有限的环境中,我们的模型可用于快速识别高优先级案例以进行进一步调查。要点 XGBoost 是用于识别 禽流感 病例是否人畜共患的二元分类任务的性能最佳的机器学习算法。我们的模型表明,我们考虑的所有八种蛋白质都在确定 禽流感 病例是否具有人畜共患潜力方面发挥作用。禽流感 蛋白的遗传组成特征可以编码可用作人畜共患病特征的信息。我们创建了一个系统,可以识别仍然存在于禽类 宿主 中的高优先级病例,这些病例应优先进行进一步调查。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 在野生水禽中广泛传播,但偶尔会跨越物种屏障感染人类,由于 死亡率 较高和 传播 继续传播的可能性,构成大流行风险。该研究解决了对工具的需求,以在人类感染发生之前预测哪些 AIV 病例可能导致 人兽共患病。
设计与对象
该研究采用一种无需比对的计算方法,根据八种病毒蛋白的遗传组成特征,将 AIV 病例分为人畜共患病或非人畜共患病。它采用机器学习算法(包括 XGBoost)来构建针对每种蛋白质和组合模型优化的分类模型。
核心方法
作者从八种 AIV 蛋白中提取了遗传组成特征,包括二肽组成和氨基酸理化特性,如电荷、二级结构和疏水性。优化了十个分类模型:每个蛋白质一个,两个组合模型,其中 XGBoost 被认为是性能最佳的算法。
主要结果
该研究一致发现,在将 AIV 病例分类为人畜共患病或非人畜共患病方面,XGBoost 的表现优于其他算法。所有八种蛋白质均被鉴定为在预测人畜共患病 传播 中发挥作用,其中 PB2 和 HA 蛋白质根据特定的氨基酸理化特性被强调为特别重要。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明 AIV 蛋白的遗传组成特征可以编码人畜共患病特征,从而能够预测人畜共患病的可能性。新颖之处在于多模型方法和特定蛋白质特征(例如 PB2 和 HA 中的蛋白质特征)的识别作为关键指标。
科研与公卫意义
该模型可以优先考虑禽类 宿主 中基因最接近人畜共患病例的 AIV 病例,从而能够快速识别高优先级病例,以便在资源有限的环境中进行进一步调查。这种方法可以改进 监测 和潜在人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件的风险评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究无需计算和比对,依赖于遗传特征,而不是人畜共患潜力的实验验证。由于缺乏体内或临床验证以及对不同 AIV 毒株的普遍适用性的潜在限制,证据强度中等。

Predicting the Risk of Avian Influenza Zoonosis using Viral Genome Sequencing Data

Authors: Anna G Fairweather, Austin Andrews, Joshua Grier, Liam Brierley, Lorenzo Cattarino, Panovsk-Griffiths J, Jasmina Panovska‐Griffiths, Jasmina Panovska-Griffiths
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Avian Influenza viruses (AIVs) infect a broad host range despite having a natural reservoir in wild aquatic birds. Whilst most strains stay within their host species, some break the species barrier through genetic adaptations. We are most concerned about zoonotic cases, where a human becomes infected. Despite these events being rare, they are associated with high mortality and introduce the risk of onward human-to-human transmission of AIV. As a novel pathogen within the human population, this could have pandemic potential. Using genetic composition features for 8 AIV proteins drawn from viral sequence data, we employ machine-learning algorithms to classify AIV cases as zoonotic or not. These genetic features encode host ‘signatures’ which can indicate zoonosis and include frequency measures such as dipeptide composition and amino acid physiochemical properties. We consistently find XGBoost to outperform all other algorithms. We optimise parameters for ten classification models: one for each of the 8 proteins and two combined models. Following this, we show that a multi-model approach gives the best performing prediction for AIV zoonosis. We have identified all 8 proteins as having a role in predicting zoonotic transmission. Of particular importance is the PB2 and HA proteins, with specific amino acid physiochemical properties such as charge, secondary structure and hydrophobicity amongst the most indicative features in our combined models. Our alignment-free computational study can identify AIV cases still within avian hosts which are genetically closest to zoonotic AIV cases, thereby identifying the cases most likely to cross the species barrier. In a resource limited environment, our model could be used to quickly identify high priority cases for further investigation. Key points XGBoost is the best performing machine learning algorithm for the binary classification task of identifying avian influenza cases as zoonotic or not. Our model suggests that all eight proteins we considered have a role in determining whether avian influenza cases have zoonotic potential or not. Genetic composition features of avian influenza proteins can encode information that can be used as zoonotic signatures. We have created a system that identifies high priority cases still within avian hosts that should be prioritised for further investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian Influenza viruses (AIVs) circulate widely in wild aquatic birds but occasionally cross species barriers to infect humans, posing pandemic risks due to high mortality and potential for onward transmission. The study addresses the need for tools to predict which AIV cases are likely to cause zoonosis before human infection occurs.
Design and population
The study uses a computational, alignment-free approach to classify AIV cases as zoonotic or non-zoonotic based on genetic composition features of eight viral proteins. It employs machine-learning algorithms, including XGBoost, to build classification models optimized for each protein and combined models.
Core methods
The authors extracted genetic composition features from eight AIV proteins, including dipeptide composition and amino acid physiochemical properties such as charge, secondary structure, and hydrophobicity. Ten classification models were optimized: one for each protein and two combined models, with XGBoost identified as the best-performing algorithm.
Main results
The study consistently found XGBoost to outperform other algorithms in classifying AIV cases as zoonotic or not. All eight proteins were identified as having a role in predicting zoonotic transmission, with PB2 and HA proteins highlighted as particularly important based on specific amino acid physiochemical properties.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that genetic composition features of AIV proteins can encode zoonotic signatures, enabling prediction of zoonotic potential. The novelty lies in the multi-model approach and the identification of specific protein features, such as those in PB2 and HA, as key indicators.
Scientific and public-health significance
The model can prioritize AIV cases within avian hosts that are genetically closest to zoonotic cases, enabling rapid identification of high-priority cases for further investigation in resource-limited settings. This approach may improve surveillance and risk assessment for potential zoonotic spillover events.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is computational and alignment-free, relying on genetic features rather than experimental validation of zoonotic potential. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of in vivo or clinical validation and potential limitations in generalizability to diverse AIV strains.

禽流感 数学模型中的多稳定性、混沌和瞬态动力学

Multistability, chaos, and transient dynamics in an avian influenza mathematical model
作者： Ana Luiza R. de Moraes, Fátima E. Cruziniani, Enrique C. Gabrick, Antonio M. Batista, Iberê L. Caldas, Jürgen Kurths
摘要中文翻译摘要 流感,俗称流感,是一种高度传染性的呼吸道感染,会导致感染者出现咳嗽、疼痛和不适等症状。甲型流感感染的一种重要形式是由 H5N1 病毒引起的 死亡率,由于其高 死亡率 发病率,自二十世纪末以来一直受到关注。由于 禽流感 具有感染其他哺乳动物的能力以及 跨物种溢出 对人类的潜在风险,监测和控制 跨物种溢出 对于公共卫生问题非常重要。人们提出了能够描述 禽流感 动态的数学模型,以探索该疾病的传播并采取控制措施来遏制该疾病。在这项工作中,我们分析了 禽流感 模型的动态行为,该模型由两个耦合的 SI(易感感染)模型组成,一个描述鸟类种群,另一个描述人类种群。使用鸟 死亡率 速率作为控制参数和滞后型分岔图,我们识别了较宽参数范围内的双稳态。随着该参数的增加,我们观察到大量的超混沌。此外,我们还发现并量化了一组给定参数值的瞬态混沌和边界危机。这些发现有助于更详细地理解 禽流感 传播 数学模型中可能出现的非线性动力学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索 禽流感 传播 的复杂动力学及其对人类的潜力,强调需要更好地了解该疾病的行为以改进控制策略。
设计与对象
该研究采用由两个耦合的 SI 模型组成的数学模型来模拟 死亡率 在鸟类和人类群体中的传播,重点研究鸟类 死亡率 率对疾病动态的影响。
核心方法
作者使用滞后型分岔图来分析模型的行为,识别鸟 死亡率 速率变化时的双稳态和超混沌,并量化特定参数值的瞬态混沌和边界危机。
主要结果
该研究观察了多种鸟类 死亡率 速率的双稳态,并随着参数的增加确定了显着范围的超混沌。对于给定的一组参数,瞬态混沌和边界危机也被量化。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了 禽流感 传播 的非线性动力学,为该疾病的复杂行为以及不同条件下多种稳定状态的潜力提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为开发更准确的数学模型提供信息,用于预测 禽流感 暴发 并改进疾病遏制的公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于数学模型,未提供实证数据,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于分析的理论性质,证据的强度中等。

Multistability, chaos, and transient dynamics in an avian influenza mathematical model

Authors: Ana Luiza R. de Moraes, Fátima E. Cruziniani, Enrique C. Gabrick, Antonio M. Batista, Iberê L. Caldas, Jürgen Kurths
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Influenza, commonly known as the flu, is a highly contagious respiratory infection that causes symptoms, such as cough, aches, and malaise in infected individuals. One important form of influenza A infection is avian influenza, caused by the H5N1 virus, which has been a concern since the late twentieth century due to its high mortality rate. Monitoring and controlling avian influenza is of great importance for public health issues due to its ability to infect other mammals and its potential risk for spillover to humans. Mathematical models capable of describing the dynamics of avian influenza have been proposed to explore the spread of the disease and adapt control measures to contain it. In this work, we analyze the dynamical behavior of an avian influenza model consisting of two coupled SI (susceptible-infected) models, one describing the bird population and the other the human population. Using the bird mortality rate as the control parameter and hysteresis-type bifurcation diagrams, we identify bistability over a wide parameter range. As this parameter increases, we observe a significant range of hyperchaos. Furthermore, we uncover and quantify transient chaos and boundary crises for a given set of parameter values. These findings contribute to a more detailed understanding of the non-linear dynamics that may arise in mathematical models of avian influenza transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the complex dynamics of avian influenza transmission and its potential for spillover to humans, highlighting the need for a better understanding of the disease's behavior to improve control strategies.
Design and population
The research employed a mathematical model consisting of two coupled SI models to simulate the spread of avian influenza within bird and human populations, focusing on the impact of bird mortality rates on disease dynamics.
Core methods
The authors used hysteresis-type bifurcation diagrams to analyze the model's behavior, identifying bistability and hyperchaos as the bird mortality rate varied, and quantified transient chaos and boundary crises for specific parameter values.
Main results
The study observed bistability over a wide range of bird mortality rates and identified a significant range of hyperchaos as the parameter increased. Transient chaos and boundary crises were also quantified for a given set of parameters.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal the non-linear dynamics of avian influenza transmission, offering new insights into the complex behavior of the disease and the potential for multiple stable states under varying conditions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of more accurate mathematical models for predicting avian influenza outbreaks and improving public health strategies for disease containment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a mathematical model and does not provide empirical data, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the theoretical nature of the analysis.

针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的牛顿差分优化

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2
作者： Siti Amiroch
摘要中文翻译禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。本研究提出了牛顿除差(NDD)优化,这是一种轻量级正对角曲率感知训练策略,作为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。NDD 在支架密钥和相似性聚类协议下的 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC(接收者操作特征 - 曲线下面积)和 PR-AUC(曲线下精确召回面积),并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。这项工作的新颖性在于首次系统评估 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在稀疏高维配体筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案,尽管在实际 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前仍然需要外部验证和前瞻性实验确认。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A/H9N2 造成持续的人畜共患病和兽医威胁,但用于 抗病毒药物 化合物优先级排序的有效计算工具仍然不发达,特别是在高维神经筛选中的​​优化器行为方面。
设计与对象
本研究提出牛顿差分(NDD)优化作为一种​​针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的基于指纹的神经虚拟筛选的新型优化器,目的是评估其在对齐的分子指纹表示和化学结构验证协议中的性能。
核心方法
由 615 种候选活性化合物和 844 种诱饵组成的 1,459 个分子的对齐基准由 EState(79 个特征)、PubChem(881 个特征)和 Klekota–Roth(4,860 个特征)指纹代表,共享相同的分子身份、标签和分割分配。固定多层感知器 (MLP) 分类器使用 NDD 和七个基线优化器在分层、支架键和相似性聚类分割协议下跨五个重复种子进行训练。
主要结果
NDD 在支架关键和相似性聚类协议下在 Klekota–Roth 指纹上实现了最高的描述性 ROC-AUC 和 PR-AUC,并且在分层分割下保持竞争力,ROC-AUC 为 0.9872。架构敏感性测试证实了跨多种网络配置的稳定 NDD 性能,ROC-AUC 值范围为 0.9876 至 0.9890。与无 Hessian 优化相比,在相同的仅 CPU 配置下,NDD 将 Klekota-Roth 上的每次运行时间从大约 50-54 秒减少到大约 8 秒,同时实现了相当的排名性能。
解释与创新
这项工作的新颖性在于首次对 H9N2 神经虚拟筛选的 NDD 进行系统评估,证明正对角线曲率感知缩放在基于稀疏高维配体的筛选设置中提供了实用、稳定且计算高效的优化替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为针对 禽流感 A/H9N2 的 抗病毒药物 化合物优先排序提供了一种计算有效的优化策略,这可以加速针对这种人畜共患威胁的潜在治疗方法的发现。
局限与证据强度
在实施实际的 抗病毒药物 优先级排序之前,外部验证和前瞻性实验确认仍然是必要的。由于研究的计算性质和实验验证的需要,证据强度中等。

Newton Divided Difference Optimization for Fingerprint-Based Neural Virtual Screening against Avian Influenza A/H9N2

Authors: Siti Amiroch
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening. This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization, a lightweight positive diagonal curvature-aware training strategy, as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols. An aligned benchmark of 1,459 molecules consisting of 615 candidate active compounds and 844 decoys was represented by EState (79 features), PubChem (881 features), and Klekota–Roth (4,860 features) fingerprints, sharing identical molecule identities, labels, and split assignments. A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds. NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC (Receiver Operating Characteriztic – Area Under the Curve) and PR-AUC (Precision-Recall Area Under the Curve) on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Architecture-sensitivity tests confirmed stable NDD performance across multiple network configurations, with ROC-AUC values ranging from 0.9876 to 0.9890. Compared with Hessian-free optimization, NDD reduced per-run runtime from approximately 50–54 seconds to approximately 8 seconds on Klekota–Roth under the same CPU-only configuration while achieving comparable ranking performance. The novelty of this work lies in the first systematic assessment of NDD for H9N2 neural virtual screening, demonstrating that positive diagonal curvature-aware scaling provides a practical, stable, and computationally efficient optimization alternative in sparse high-dimensional ligand-based screening settings, although external validation and prospective experimental confirmation remain necessary before practical antiviral prioritization.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A/H9N2 poses persistent zoonotic and veterinary threats, yet efficient computational tools for antiviral compound prioritization remain underdeveloped, particularly with respect to optimizer behavior in high-dimensional neural screening.
Design and population
This study proposes Newton Divided Difference (NDD) optimization as a novel optimizer for fingerprint-based neural virtual screening against avian influenza A/H9N2, with the objective of evaluating its performance across aligned molecular fingerprint representations and chemically structured validation protocols.

A fixed multilayer perceptron (MLP) classifier was trained with NDD and seven baseline optimizers under stratified, scaffold-key, and similarity-cluster split protocols across five repeated seeds.
Main results
NDD achieved the highest descriptive ROC-AUC and PR-AUC on Klekota–Roth fingerprints under scaffold-key and similarity-cluster protocols, and remained competitive under the stratified split with ROC-AUC of 0.9872. Limitations and evidence strength

山猫 (Lynx rufus) 中 高致病性禽流感 A (H5N1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测,华盛顿,2025 年

Co-detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ), Washington, 2025
作者： Hayley D. Yaglom, Zachary A. Barrand, Anna Schwabe, Katy L. Parise, Alysha Evans, Megan A. Dietz, Laura Williams, Beth Nielsen, Paige Hawkinson, Daryn E. Erickson
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 是重要的 One Health 病原体,其范围不断扩大。我们描述了对华盛顿州山猫 (Lynx rufus) 的一项调查,其中同时检测到 HPAI H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 D1.1)和 SARS-CoV-2。临床症状、诊断结果和病理学与H5N1感染一致。本报告强调了综合野生动物 监测 对于表征人类与野生动物界面中新出现的人畜共患病原体的重要性。重要性 本报告记录了在野生动物康复机构收治的一只山猫身上同时检测到两种 One Health 威胁:禽流感 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2。虽然很少见,但同时检测到这些病毒凸显了哺乳动物常规监测中的差距,并说明了野生动物如何充当人与动物界面上病原体的桥梁 宿主。加强协调一致的 监测 工作对于了解疾病动态、保护动物和公共卫生以及支持更广泛的“同一个健康”准备策略至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查山猫体内 HPAI A(H5N1) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测,强调这些重要的 One Health 病原体不断扩大的 宿主 范围。
设计与对象
该研究涉及华盛顿州的一只山猫 (Lynx rufus),在野生动物康复设施的调查中同时检测到 HPAI H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b(基因型 D1.1)和 SARS-CoV-2。
核心方法
该调查包括临床体征、诊断结果和病理学分析,以评估山猫体内是否存在 HPAI H5N1 和 SARS-CoV-2。
主要结果
临床症状、诊断结果和病理学与 H5N1 感染一致,表明山猫体内同时检测到 HPAI H5N1 和 SARS-CoV-2。
解释与创新
在山猫体内共同检测到这些病毒强调了整合 监测 的必要性,以更好地了解人类与野生动物界面中新出现的人畜共患病原体。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现强调了监测哺乳动物 禽流感 和 SARS-CoV-2 的重要性,以识别潜在的桥接 宿主 并提高对人畜共患疾病 暴发 的防范能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个山猫病例,这可能会限制普遍性,但由于联合检测的直接观察和一致的临床结果,证据是强有力的。

Co-detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ), Washington, 2025

Authors: Hayley D. Yaglom, Zachary A. Barrand, Anna Schwabe, Katy L. Parise, Alysha Evans, Megan A. Dietz, Laura Williams, Beth Nielsen, Paige Hawkinson, Daryn E. Erickson
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) are important One Health pathogens with expanding host ranges. We describe an investigation of a bobcat ( Lynx rufus ) in Washington State in which HPAI H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b (genotype D1.1) and SARS-CoV-2 were co-detected. Clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology were consistent with H5N1 infection. This report highlights the importance of integrated wildlife surveillance to characterize emerging zoonotic pathogens at the human-wildlife interface. IMPORTANCE This report documents the co-detection of two One Health threats, avian influenza and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2, in a bobcat admitted to a wildlife rehabilitation facility. Although rare, the simultaneous detection of these viruses highlights gaps in routine monitoring of mammals and illustrates how wildlife may serve as bridging hosts for pathogens across the human-animal interface. Strengthening coordinated surveillance efforts is essential for understanding disease dynamics, protecting animal and public health, and supporting broader One Health preparedness strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the co-detection of HPAI A(H5N1) and SARS-CoV-2 in a bobcat, highlighting the expanding host ranges of these important One Health pathogens.
Design and population
The study involved a bobcat (Lynx rufus) in Washington State, where HPAI H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b (genotype D1.1) and SARS-CoV-2 were simultaneously detected during an investigation at a wildlife rehabilitation facility.
Core methods
The investigation included clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology analysis to assess the presence of HPAI H5N1 and SARS-CoV-2 in the bobcat.
Main results
Clinical signs, diagnostic findings, and pathology were consistent with H5N1 infection, indicating the co-detection of HPAI H5N1 and SARS-CoV-2 in the bobcat.
Interpretation and novelty
The co-detection of these viruses in a bobcat underscores the need for integrated surveillance to better understand emerging zoonotic pathogens at the human-wildlife interface.
Scientific and public-health significance
This finding emphasizes the importance of monitoring mammals for avian influenza and SARS-CoV-2 to identify potential bridging hosts and improve preparedness for zoonotic disease outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single bobcat case, which may limit generalizability, but the evidence is strong due to the direct observation of co-detection and consistent clinical findings.

没有孤岛的 监测:西非 禽流感 的单一健康数据势在必行

Surveillance without silos: a One Health data imperative for avian influenza in West Africa
作者： Emmanuel Musa, Francis Akpa, Godfred O. Boateng
摘要中文翻译自 2006 年尼日利亚出现首例人类病例以来,高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 就在西非传播,使该地区成为撒哈拉以南非洲多种病毒谱系的关键进入点和传播中心。推动 禽流感 在全球传播的 2.3.4.4b 病毒分支现在更频繁地感染哺乳动物,这是一个令人不安的迹象,增加了未来大流行的风险。与此同时,西非控制高致病性禽流感的能力因零散的 监测 系统而受到阻碍。动物、人类和环境卫生服务各自独立运作,依赖不兼容的数据格式,并由不同的机构管理,这不利于早期发现和快速反应。本文描述了西非现有的 监测 孤岛,并概述了未能整合它们的后果,包括延迟检测未来的人类或动物健康威胁。它重点介绍了 One Health 监测 在尼日利亚、塞内加尔、几内亚、贝宁和加纳所做努力的新例子。从操作角度来看,早期预警和及时响应有五个必要条件:可互操作的卫生信息系统;跨部门共同治理;可持续融资;与家禽饲养社区的密切沟通;以及有效的跨境合作。建立完全集成的、全系统的国家 监测 平台对于有效的准备是必不可少的。如果没有这种一体化,西非国家将继续发现和应对新出现的健康威胁,但为时已晚。鉴于该地区反复出现的人畜共患病 跨物种溢出 和不断扩大的生态脆弱性,将数据集成纳入防备策略的核心对于减轻未来流行病的风险至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究解决了对集成 监测 系统来监测西非 禽流感 的需求,该病毒自 2006 年以来一直在西非传播,由于其能够感染哺乳动物,因此对未来的大流行构成重大风险。
设计与对象
该研究对西非当前的 监测 系统进行了描述性分析,重点关注该地区作为 禽流感 谱系的关键入口点和传播中心的作用。它研究了尼日利亚、塞内加尔、几内亚、贝宁和加纳动物、人类和环境卫生服务的分散系统的影响。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对西非现有 监测 实践、数据格式和机构结构的审查,强调缺乏跨部门的互操作性和共享治理。
主要结果
结果显示,西非的监测系统分散,动物、人类和环境卫生服务孤立运行,依赖不兼容的数据格式并由不同的机构管理,这阻碍了早期发现和快速响应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,目前 监测 的孤立方法是有效防范大流行病的关键障碍,他们强调需要集成系统来解决这一差距。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明了整合 监测 系统以改善对 禽流感 威胁的早期检测和响应的重要性,这对于减轻该地区未来流行病的风险至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其描述性性质和对现有报告的依赖而受到限制,但由于对 监测 挑战的清晰记录以及对系统集成作为解决方案的强调,证据是强有力的。

Surveillance without silos: a One Health data imperative for avian influenza in West Africa

Authors: Emmanuel Musa, Francis Akpa, Godfred O. Boateng
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) has circulated in West Africa since Nigeria’s first human case in 2006, making the region a key entry point and dispersal hub for multiple virus lineages in sub-Saharan Africa. The 2.3.4.4b virus clade driving the global spread of avian influenza is now infecting mammals more often, a troubling sign that raises the risk of a future pandemic. At the same time, West Africa’s ability to control HPAI is held back by fragmented surveillance systems. Animal, human and environmental health services operate in isolation, rely on incompatible data formats and are managed by separate institutions, which undermines early detection and rapid response. This paper describes the existing surveillance silos in West Africa and outlines the consequences of failing to integrate them, including delayed detection of future human or animal health threats. It highlights emerging examples of One Health surveillance efforts in Nigeria, Senegal, Guinea, Benin and Ghana. From an operational standpoint, five conditions are essential for early warning and timely response: interoperable health information systems; shared governance across sectors; sustainable financing; strong communication with poultry-keeping communities; and effective cross-border collaboration. Establishing fully integrated, system-wide national surveillance platforms is indispensable for effective preparedness. Without such integration, West African countries will continue to detect and respond to emerging health threats too late. Given the region’s recurrent zoonotic spillover and expanding ecological vulnerabilities, embedding data integration at the core of preparedness strategies is essential for mitigating the risk of future pandemics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the need for integrated surveillance systems to monitor avian influenza in West Africa, where the virus has circulated since 2006 and poses a significant risk for future pandemics due to its ability to infect mammals.
Design and population
The study is a descriptive analysis of the current surveillance systems in West Africa, focusing on the region’s role as a key entry point and dispersal hub for avian influenza lineages. It examines the impact of fragmented systems across animal, human and environmental health services in Nigeria, Senegal, Guinea, Benin and Ghana.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of existing surveillance practices, data formats and institutional structures in West Africa, highlighting the lack of interoperability and shared governance across sectors.
Main results
The results show that surveillance systems in West Africa are fragmented, with animal, human and environmental health services operating in isolation, relying on incompatible data formats and managed by separate institutions, which hinders early detection and rapid response.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the current siloed approach to surveillance is a critical barrier to effective pandemic preparedness, and they emphasize the need for integrated systems to address this gap.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of integrating surveillance systems to improve early detection and response to avian influenza threats, which is crucial for mitigating the risk of future pandemics in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its descriptive nature and reliance on existing reports, but the evidence is strong due to the clear documentation of surveillance challenges and the emphasis on system integration as a solution.

从印度散养蛋鸭中分离出的分支 2.3.4.4b 高致病性 H5N1 禽流感 病毒的遗传和抗原特征

Genetic and Antigenic Characterization of a Clade 2.3.4.4b Highly Pathogenic H5N1 Avian Influenza Virus Isolated from a Free-Range Layer Duck in India
作者： Kumar Gaurav, Manoj Kumar, Shanmugasundaram Nagarajan, Harshad V. Murugkar, Pushpendra K. Namdeo, Deepak K. Pankaj, Sonam Agarwal, Aniket Sanyal, Chakradhar Tosh
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒对全球家禽业构成重大威胁。该报告强调了 2022 年从印度喀拉拉邦库塔纳德三角洲的一只散养蛋鸭中分离出来的分支 2.3.4.4b HPAI H5N1 病毒的遗传和抗原特征。该病毒与 2021 年 10 月在俄罗斯检测到的 H5N1 病毒相似,在抗原上与分支 2.3.2 不同,显示血凝抑制滴度降低了 16 倍,并且HA 抗原位点的氨基酸差异为 23.95%。鸭病毒与欧亚 野生鸟类 中发现的 H5N1 病毒具有遗传相似性,表明这些鸟类在其引入过程中发挥了作用。该研究强调了加强 禽流感 监测 对监测病毒 进化 和传播的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 病毒对全球家禽业构成重大威胁。该研究旨在鉴定从印度散养蛋鸭中新分离出的 HPAI H5N1 病毒的特征。
设计与对象
该研究于 2022 年从印度喀拉拉邦库塔纳德三角洲的一只散养蛋鸭中分离出分支 2.3.4.4b HPAI H5N1 病毒。对该病毒的遗传和抗原特征进行了分析。
核心方法
该病毒与 2021 年 10 月在俄罗斯检测到的 H5N1 病毒进行了比较,并分析了抗原差异。评估与欧亚 野生鸟类 H5N1 病毒的遗传相似性,以推断潜在的 传播 途径。
主要结果
与进化枝2.3.2相比,鸭病毒的血凝抑制滴度降低了16倍,并且HA抗原位点的氨基酸差异为23.95%。它与欧亚 野生鸟类 中发现的 H5N1 病毒具有遗传相似性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,欧亚 野生鸟类 可能在这种 HPAI H5N1 病毒引入鸭群的过程中发挥了作用。这项研究为印度 HPAI H5N1 病毒的遗传和抗原多样性提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要增强 禽流感 病毒的 监测 来监测其 进化 和传播,这对于在家禽中预防 暴发 和减轻人畜共患风险至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,研究结果基于遗传和抗原特征的直接比较,支持适度的置信度评估。

Genetic and Antigenic Characterization of a Clade 2.3.4.4b Highly Pathogenic H5N1 Avian Influenza Virus Isolated from a Free-Range Layer Duck in India

Authors: Kumar Gaurav, Manoj Kumar, Shanmugasundaram Nagarajan, Harshad V. Murugkar, Pushpendra K. Namdeo, Deepak K. Pankaj, Sonam Agarwal, Aniket Sanyal, Chakradhar Tosh
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 virus poses a significant threat to the global poultry sector. The report highlights the genetic and antigenic features of a clade 2.3.4.4b HPAI H5N1 virus isolated from a free-range layer duck in the Kuttanad delta of Kerala, India, in 2022. The virus is similar to an H5N1 virus detected in Russia in October 2021 and antigenically distinct from clade 2.3.2, showing a 16-fold reduction in hemagglutination inhibition titer and 23.95% amino acid differences at the HA antigenic sites. The duck virus shares genetic similarities with H5N1 viruses found in Eurasian wild birds, suggesting a role for these birds in its introduction. The study underscores the importance of strengthening avian influenza surveillance to monitor virus evolution and spread.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 virus poses a significant threat to the global poultry sector. The study aimed to characterize a newly isolated HPAI H5N1 virus from a free-range layer duck in India.
Design and population
The study involved the isolation of a clade 2.3.4.4b HPAI H5N1 virus from a free-range layer duck in the Kuttanad delta of Kerala, India, in 2022. The virus was analyzed for its genetic and antigenic features.
Core methods
The virus was compared to an H5N1 virus detected in Russia in October 2021 and analyzed for antigenic differences. Genetic similarities with Eurasian wild bird H5N1 viruses were assessed to infer potential transmission routes.
Main results
The duck virus showed a 16-fold reduction in hemagglutination inhibition titer compared to clade 2.3.2 and had 23.95% amino acid differences at the HA antigenic sites. It shared genetic similarities with H5N1 viruses found in Eurasian wild birds.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Eurasian wild birds may play a role in the introduction of this HPAI H5N1 virus into the duck population. This study provides new insights into the genetic and antigenic diversity of HPAI H5N1 viruses in India.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the need for enhanced surveillance of avian influenza viruses to monitor their evolution and spread, which is crucial for preventing outbreaks in poultry and mitigating zoonotic risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the findings are grounded in direct comparisons of genetic and antigenic features, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

通过免疫信息学方法设计 H5N1 流感多表位 疫苗

H5N1 influenza multi-epitope vaccine design through immunoinformatics approaches
作者： Ankita Singh, Omer S. Alkhnbashi, Filippo Castiglione
摘要中文翻译H5N1 禽流感 病毒对动物和人类健康构成重大威胁,极有可能引发全球大流行。本研究概述了一种分步免疫信息学驱动的方法来设计针对 H5N1 的多表位 疫苗。通过分析保守的 H5N1 蛋白(包括血凝素、神经氨酸酶以及基质蛋白 1 和 2)的序列来鉴定潜在的 疫苗 候选物。预测 B 细胞表位和 T 细胞肽,然后筛选毒性和过敏性。最终的 疫苗 构造已经过结构稳定性验证,并且计算机模拟已用于评估其产生持久 免疫应答 的理论潜力。这种计算机方法利用一套计算工具,通过优先考虑有希望的候选者进行实验验证来加速 疫苗 设计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 H5N1 禽流感 病毒造成的公共卫生威胁以及 疫苗 快速开发的需求。它询问是否可以设计针对 H5N1 的免疫信息学驱动的多表位 疫苗。
设计与对象
该研究采用计算机免疫信息学方法设计针对 H5N1 的多表位 疫苗。没有人类或动物参与者、临床标本或湿实验室验证的报告。
核心方法
对保守的 H5N1 蛋白(血凝素、神经氨酸酶、基质蛋白 1 和 2)进行分析,以确定潜在的 疫苗 候选者。预测 B 细胞和 T 细胞表位,筛选毒性和过敏原性,并使用计算机模拟评估最终 疫苗 构建体的结构稳定性和理论免疫原性。
主要结果
最终的 疫苗 构造经过了结构稳定性验证,并通过计算机模拟评估了其产生持久 免疫应答 的理论潜力。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明免疫信息学驱动的针对 H5N1 的多表位 疫苗 可以通过计算设计和结构验证。他们强调了使用逐步的计算机管道来确定候选者的优先级以供未来实验验证的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,计算 疫苗 设计可以加速识别有前景的 H5N1 疫苗 候选者,从而有可能减少实验验证所需的时间和成本。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于计算机预测和结构建模,没有实验验证或临床数据。由于缺乏生物学或临床证实,证据的强度较低。

H5N1 influenza multi-epitope vaccine design through immunoinformatics approaches

Authors: Ankita Singh, Omer S. Alkhnbashi, Filippo Castiglione
Original abstract (English)
The H5N1 avian influenza virus poses a significant threat to both animal and human health, with a high potential for triggering a global pandemic. This study outlines a step-by-step immunoinformatics-driven approach to design a multi-epitope vaccine against H5N1. Potential vaccine candidates were identified by analyzing the sequences of conserved H5N1 proteins, including Hemagglutinin, Neuraminidase, and Matrix protein 1 and 2. B-cell epitopes and T-cell peptides were predicted and then screened for toxicity and allergenicity. The final vaccine construct has been verified for structural stability and computer simulation has been used to evaluate its theoretical potential for eliciting a long-lasting immune response. This In silico approach leverages a suite of computational tools to accelerate vaccine design by prioritizing promising candidates for experimental validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the public-health threat posed by H5N1 avian influenza virus and the need for rapid vaccine development. It asks whether an immunoinformatics-driven multi-epitope vaccine can be designed against H5N1.
Design and population
The study employs an in silico immunoinformatics approach to design a multi-epitope vaccine targeting H5N1. No human or animal participants, clinical specimens, or wet-lab validation are reported.
Core methods
Conserved H5N1 proteins (Hemagglutinin, Neuraminidase, Matrix protein 1 and 2) were analyzed to identify potential vaccine candidates. B-cell and T-cell epitopes were predicted, screened for toxicity and allergenicity, and a final vaccine construct was evaluated for structural stability and theoretical immunogenicity using computer simulations.
Main results
The final vaccine construct was verified for structural stability and its theoretical potential to elicit a long-lasting immune response was evaluated via computer simulation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating that an immunoinformatics-driven multi-epitope vaccine against H5N1 can be computationally designed and structurally validated. They highlight the novelty of using a step-by-step in silico pipeline to prioritize candidates for future experimental validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that computational vaccine design may accelerate the identification of promising H5N1 vaccine candidates, potentially reducing the time and cost required for experimental validation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in silico predictions and structural modeling, with no experimental validation or clinical data. The strength of the evidence is low due to the absence of biological or clinical confirmation.

2026 年 6 月至 7 月,澳大利亚新南威尔士州亨特新英格兰地区首次出现高致病性 禽流感 A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 入侵

The first high pathogenicity avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b incursions in the Hunter New England region, New South Wales, Australia, June–July 2026
作者： Emily Gibson, Meg Whitley, Renée Reynolds, Megan Vidler, Kristy Crooks, Peter Murray
摘要中文翻译2026 年 6 月 28 日和 2026 年 7 月 10 日,在澳大利亚新南威尔士州亨特新英格兰地区发现了两次高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 入侵鸟类。这是新南威尔士州首次检测到该病毒,发生在 2026 年 6 月澳大利亚首次检测到该病毒后不久。新英格兰亨特人口健康中心该部门使用该部门专门设计和构建的接触管理系统来管理受感染鸟类的人类接触。我们报告公共卫生应对措施和改进机会。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在识别并记录高致病性 禽流感 A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 在澳大利亚新南威尔士州亨特新英格兰地区的首次入侵,以了解这些事件对公共卫生的影响。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注了2026年6月28日和2026年7月10日澳大利亚新南威尔士州亨特新英格兰地区的两次HPAI A(H5N1)分支2.3.4.4b对流浪鸟类的入侵。这是新南威尔士州首次检测到该病毒,发生在2026年6月澳大利亚首次检测到该病毒后不久。
核心方法
新英格兰亨特人口健康部门使用该部门专门设计和开发的接触管理系统来管理受感染鸟类的人类接触。
主要结果
该研究报告了公共卫生对这些入侵的反应,强调了管理和遏制策略的改进机会。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为加强 禽流感 暴发 公共卫生准备和响应机制的机会,强调需要改进方案。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对澳大利亚 HPAI A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 入侵的初步反应的见解,这可以为未来的 监测 和遏制工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于仅摘要的报告,这限制了分析的深度和对所描述的详细公共卫生应对策略的信心。

The first high pathogenicity avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b incursions in the Hunter New England region, New South Wales, Australia, June–July 2026

Authors: Emily Gibson, Meg Whitley, Renée Reynolds, Megan Vidler, Kristy Crooks, Peter Murray
Original abstract (English)
Two incursions of high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b in vagrant birds were identified in the Hunter New England region of New South Wales, Australia on 28 June 2026 and 10 July 2026. These were the first detections of the virus in New South Wales and occurred shortly after the first Australian detection in June 2026. The Hunter New England Population Health Unit managed human contacts of the infected birds using a contact management system designed and purpose-built by the Unit. We report on the public health response and opportunities for improvement.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify and document the first incursions of high pathogenicity avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b in the Hunter New England region of New South Wales, Australia, to understand the public health implications of these events.
Design and population
The study focused on two incursions of HPAI A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b in vagrant birds in the Hunter New England region, New South Wales, Australia, on 28 June 2026 and 10 July 2026. These were the first detections of the virus in New South Wales and occurred shortly after the first Australian detection in June 2026.
Core methods
The Hunter New England Population Health Unit managed human contacts of the infected birds using a contact management system specifically designed and developed by the Unit.
Main results
The study reported on the public health response to these incursions, highlighting opportunities for improvement in the management and containment strategies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as an opportunity to enhance public health preparedness and response mechanisms for avian influenza outbreaks, emphasizing the need for improved protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the initial response to HPAI A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b incursions in Australia, which can inform future surveillance and containment efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only report, which limits the depth of analysis and the confidence in the detailed public health response strategies described.

社论:推进兽医和人畜共患感染研究的测序技术

Editorial: Sequencing technologies in advancing veterinary and zoonotic infection research
作者： Henry M. Kariithi, Valerie J. Carabetta, Christina L. Leyson, Iryna V. Goraichuk
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究满足了对先进测序技术的需求,以更好地了解和管理传染病威胁,特别是那些影响牲畜、野生动物和人类健康的威胁。
设计与对象
该社论汇编了 16 篇研究论文,探讨了测序技术在兽医和人畜共患感染研究中的应用,重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒及其生态和流行病学背景。
核心方法
该社论讨论了各种测序方法,包括对已知病原体进行有针对性的高分辨率表征、更广泛的病原体不可知发现、基于平铺扩增子的测序以及非靶向宏基因组测序,从而可以在合并样本中鉴定多种遗传多样性的病毒。
主要结果
这些研究强调了测序技术恢复和表征病毒 基因组、识别重配模式以及检测新型病原体的能力,例如来自灰秃鹫的第一个编码完整的禽正鸟病毒 16 (AOAV-16) 基因组。
解释与创新
作者解释说,基因组测序为病原体的进化背景、其 宿主 范围以及人畜共患 传播 的潜力提供了重要的见解,为 监测 和疾病控制提供了新的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为制定更有效的 监测 策略、增强诊断能力并改进对 禽流感 病毒和其他人畜共患病原体的风险评估提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象级别的描述,社论没有提供详细的结果或统计分析,这降低了具体发现的可信度。

Editorial: Sequencing technologies in advancing veterinary and zoonotic infection research

Authors: Henry M. Kariithi, Valerie J. Carabetta, Christina L. Leyson, Iryna V. Goraichuk
Original abstract (English)
Sequencing as a transformative frameworkInfectious disease threats are wide-ranging, global and interconnected, affecting livestock productivity and revenue, food security, wildlife conservation, and human health. Disease emergence and transmission are driven by ecological, environmental and anthropogenic factors, including animal movement and trade, agricultural intensification, inadequate biosecurity, climate change, and wildlifelivestock-human interfaces.Integration of genomic sequencing with epidemiological surveillance and appropriate metadata generates high-resolution, actionable information. Depending on the approach and analytical objective, outputs may include genome recovery and genotyping of causative agents, characterization of mixed and coinfections, identification of mutations, reassortment and recombination events, discovery of novel pathogens, and profiling of virulence determinants (Gardy and Loman, 2018;Armstrong et al., 2019). Sequencing approaches span a continuum suited to different questions, from targeted, highresolution characterization of known pathogens to broader, pathogen-agnostic discovery (Rong et al., 2026).The 16 papers assembled in this Research Topic collectively illustrate complementary roles of sequencing technologies across this continuum, from methodological optimization and diagnostic investigations to genomic surveillance, evolutionary analyses, and biological interpretation. Together, these studies demonstrate the expanding role of sequencing technologies in advancing veterinary and zoonotic infection research.Making sequencing faster, more accessible, and fit for purposeThe value of genomic sequencing depends on the entire workflow rather than the sequencing instrument alone. Because no workflow is universally optimal, methods should be selected according to the biological question or application, sample type and quality, expected pathogen abundance, genome characteristics, required throughput and turnaround time, and available computational resources (Berry et al., 2020). Target enrichment represents another important strategy for improving sequencing efficiency. Using avian metapneumovirus as a model, Álvarez-Narváez et al. showed that tiled amplicon-based sequencing substantially improved genome recovery from samples with high host background while remaining compatible with both short-and long-read platforms. Because this approach depends on prior sequence knowledge, continued surveillance and periodic primer updates are essential as viral populations continue to evolve.Finally, Kattoor et al. demonstrated that multiplex targeted sequencing can simultaneously detect multiple pathogens associated with canine neurological and reproductive diseases while providing strain-level information. Such syndromic approaches bridge the gap between conventional singletarget PCR and untargeted metagenomics, although expanding the number of targets may reduce analytical sensitivity and increase assay complexity.Pathogen-agnostic sequencing approaches complement hypothesis-driven diagnostic methods in investigations of unresolved, atypical, multifactorial or multi-agent infectious diseases. Untargeted metagenomic sequencing is hypothesis-free in target selection, but not interpretation-free, meaning that biological and clinical judgement are essential for distinguishing true pathogens from commensals, incidental detections, and background contaminants (Miller and Chiu, 2022).The untargeted metagenomic study by Kariithi et al. demonstrated the complexity of disease-associated viral communities through co-detection and genomic characterization of multiple genetically diverse enteric viruses in pooled poultry samples, revealing distinct genomic constellations and reassortmentrelated patterns. The study illustrates that metagenomic sequencing can comprehensively define complex viral communities. However, determining the relative contribution of individual pathogens to disease and establishing causality still require integration with clinical, pathological, and epidemiological evidence. Such approaches provide a more complete etiological picture and help prioritize candidate pathogens for subsequent investigations.Beyond pathogen detection alone, genomic sequences place outbreak-associated pathogens within their evolutionary context, enabling comparison with prevailing strains and strengthening epidemiological investigations (Robinson et al., 2013) In addition to identifying the causative agent, WGS can resolve genetic lineages, spatial and temporal clustering, reassortment and recombination events, transmission pathways, introduction into and persistence within local populations, and outbreak boundaries. Collectively, these capabilities provide the genomic context needed for surveillance and disease control decisions (Struelens et al., 2024).Because migratory birds, particularly waterfowl, are natural reservoirs of avian influenza viruses (Olsen et al., 2006), surveillance of wild birds provides an early warning system for detecting viruses with potential to be introduced into poultry and, in some cases, human populations. The report by Si et al. on H5N1 and H5N9 highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses demonstrated the value of sequencing at the beginning of migration by enabling early recognition of newly introduced lineages. It further identified complete gene constellations, reassortment patterns and mutations with potential implications for host range and pathogenicity, thereby informing surveillance, diagnostic, and risk assessment efforts.Even recovery of a single isolate can substantially expand genomic knowledge, particularly for rare or poorly characterized pathogens. The incidental detection and genomic characterization of the first coding-complete Avian orthoavulavirus 16 (AOAV-16) genome from a cinereous vulture by Son et al. during influenza surveillance extended the known host range beyond wild Anseriformes, the avian order in which the virus has predominantly been detected. Considering the scarcity of AOAV -16 genomic resources, such reports expand reference databases, which are critical for whole-genomebased classifications, and improve understanding of the host range and geographical distribution of AOAV-16.Beyond expanding genomic resources for rare viruses, WGS also strengthens genomic epidemiology of zoonotic bacterial pathogens. This is illustrated by Yang Qiuju et al., who characterized the first reported human isolate of Brucella canis using comparative genomics and whole-genome SNP analysis, thereby clarifying its phylogenetic relationships and highlighting the need for enhanced surveillance. Similarly, Yang Yong et al. used integrated genomic analyses of B. abortus isolates from humans and cattle to define their population structure, phylogenetic relationships, and regional transmission patterns, illustrating how WGS strengthens surveillance and source attribution of important zoonotic bacteria.Beyond identifying and tracking pathogens, genomic sequencing can provide important insights into biological characteristics and host-pathogen interactions. However, genomic datasets predict biological potential rather than expressed phenotype or disease outcome. Their interpretation therefore requires integration with complementary laboratory, pathological, and epidemiological evidence (Balloux et al., 2018;Sheppard et al., 2018).In their study of avian pathogenic Escherichia coli O145, Tan et al. combined WGS, experimental infection, pathology, and in vivo transcriptomics to investigate pathogenicity and host adaptation.Transcriptomic comparison of bacteria colonizing duck liver with those grown in vitro identified 87 differentially expressed genes, including genes associated with metabolism and host adaptation, as well as changes in central metabolic pathways and regulatory small RNAs. The study illustrates how genomic potential can be linked to genes and pathways expressed under specific host conditions, although the biological functions of many identified transcripts remain to be experimentally validated.Wang et al. further demonstrated that multidimensional characterization of newly detected lineages benefits from the integration of genomic and phenotypic analyses. Hybrid WGS defined the phylogenetic placement, resistome, virulome, and mobile genetic elements of the novel Acinetobacter baumannii ST3475 lineage, whereas cell-based assays and animal models demonstrated enhanced biofilm formation, adhesion, invasion and pathogenicity. Although phylogenetic clustering suggested possible adaptation to the bovine mammary niche, broader surveillance and epidemiological investigations are needed to determine its zoonotic potential.Finally, Mohamed et al. synthesized evidence on virulence-associated genes in Campylobacter jejuni and C. coli from food and human sources across Gulf Cooperation Council countries. The review highlighted heterogeneous virulence profiles and important regional evidence gaps, particularly the limited number of human studies and uncertainty surrounding the relationship between gene carriage and pathogenicity. Such regional syntheses can support the development of harmonized surveillance strategies and help prioritize virulence determinants for functional investigation.The studies published in this Research Topic collectively demonstrate how sequencing has evolved beyond pathogen detection into a versatile platform supporting diagnostics, pathogen discovery, outbreak investigation, epidemiological surveillance, comparative genomics, and functional investigations. Across diverse pathogens and host species, sequencing consistently provided greater resolution than conventional diagnostic approaches while expanding understanding of pathogen diversity, evolution, and biology.An equally important lesson emerging from this collection is that genomic data are most valuable when interpreted alongside complementary clinical, pathological, phenotypic and epidemiological evidence. Likewise, the methodological studies demonstrate that successful implementation depends not only on sequencing technologies but also on optimized workflows tailored to specific diagnostic, surveillance , and research applications. These findings underscore the importance of matching sequencing strategies, analytical methods, and validation approaches to the intended application.Taken together, these studies reflect the continuing transition of sequencing from a specialized research tool to an increasingly accessible platform supporting veterinary and zoonotic infectious disease investigations. Continued progress will require optimized, standardized workflows, integration of genomic data with complementary biological and epidemiological evidence, sustained genomic surveillance, and multidisciplinary collaboration within a One Health framework. These priorities will be essential for strengthening infectious disease preparedness, detection and response to both existing and emerging disease threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for advanced sequencing technologies to better understand and manage infectious disease threats, particularly those affecting livestock, wildlife, and human health.
Design and population
The editorial compiles 16 research papers that explore the use of sequencing technologies in veterinary and zoonotic infection research, focusing on avian influenza A viruses and their ecological and epidemiological contexts.
Core methods
The editorial discusses various sequencing approaches, including targeted, high-resolution characterization of known pathogens, broader pathogen-agnostic discovery, tiled amplicon-based sequencing, and untargeted metagenomic sequencing, which allows for the identification of multiple genetically diverse viruses in pooled samples.
Main results
The studies highlight the ability of sequencing technologies to recover and characterize viral genomes, identify reassortment patterns, and detect novel pathogens, such as the first coding-complete Avian orthoavulavirus 16 (AOAV-16) genome from a cinereous vulture.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that genomic sequencing provides critical insights into the evolutionary context of pathogens, their host range, and potential for zoonotic transmission, offering novel approaches for surveillance and disease control.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the development of more effective surveillance strategies, enhance diagnostic capabilities, and improve risk assessment for avian influenza viruses and other zoonotic pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level descriptions, and the editorial does not provide detailed results or statistical analyses, which reduces the confidence in specific findings.
