Galectin-3 通过双重机制加剧甲型流感病毒 发病机制:促进血凝素依赖性进入并驱动 nlrp3 炎性体激活。

Galectin-3 exacerbates influenza a virus pathogenesis via dual mechanisms: Promoting hemagglutinin-dependent entry and driving nlrp3 inflammasome activation.
作者： Zih-Syuan Yang, Arunee Thitithanyanont, Jason C. Huang, Yen-Hsu Chen, Fu-Tong Liu, Wanchai Assavalapsakul, Sheng-Fan Wang
摘要中文翻译甲型流感病毒 (IAV),特别是 H5N1 和 H7N9 等 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 毒株,会因细胞因子风暴失调而引起严重肺炎和急性呼吸窘迫综合征 (ARDS)。这种免疫病理学的一个关键介质是 NLRP3 炎性体的过度激活,它控制着 IL-1β 的成熟。Galectin-3 (Gal3) 是一种 β-半乳糖苷结合凝集素,在感染过程中表达上调;然而,其在协调不同病毒血凝素 (HA) 亚型的 NLRP3 炎症小体激活中的具体调节作用仍有待阐明。在这项研究中,我们证明 Gal3 通过独特的双阶段机制发挥流感 发病机制 的关键放大器作用。病毒结合和内化测定的结果表明,细胞外 Gal3 通过与病毒 HA 相互作用促进病毒进入,这种作用严格依赖于毒株(H5N1 > H1N1 > H7N9)。这种增强的进入因此放大了炎症小体激活的初始信号。在细胞内,Gal3 通过维持 NF-κB 激活(启动)来上调 NLRP3 和 pro-IL-1β 转录并作为促进剂加强 NLRP3-ASC-pro-caspase-1 相互作用,促进 ASC 寡聚化(激活)来调节 NLRP3 级联。体内研究一致表明,Gal3 缺陷小鼠的存活率显着提高,体重减轻减少,感染后肺部炎症和 IL-1β 释放减弱,并且对 H5N1 具有最明显的保护作用。总的来说,这些发现将 Gal3 确定为一种有效的 宿主 因子,通过同时促进病毒进入和驱动 NLRP3 炎性体的组装来协调过度炎症,强调 Gal3-NLRP3 轴作为减轻严重流感引起的肺损伤的有前景的治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒 (IAV),包括 H5N1 和 H7N9 等 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 毒株,通过 免疫应答 失调引起严重的呼吸道疾病,特别是由过度的 NLRP3 炎性体激活引起的细胞因子风暴。该研究旨在阐明半乳糖凝集素 3 (Gal3) 在协调不同病毒血凝素 (HA) 亚型的 NLRP3 炎症小体激活中的调节作用。
设计与对象
该研究利用体外病毒结合和内化测定以及小鼠体内感染模型,研究了 Gal3 加剧甲型流感病毒 发病机制 的双重机制。该研究重点关注 Gal3 与病毒 HA 亚型(H5N1、H1N1、H7N9)之间的相互作用及其对炎症小体激活的下游影响。
核心方法
进行病毒结合和内化测定以评估细胞外 Gal3 对病毒进入不同 HA 亚型的影响。通过评估 Gal3 在维持 NF-κB 激活以上调 NLRP3 和 pro-IL-1β 转录以及促进 NLRP3-ASC-pro-caspase-1 相互作用和 ASC 寡聚化方面的作用来检查细胞内机制。
主要结果
细胞外 Gal3 以毒株依赖性方式促进病毒进入,其中对 H5N1 的作用最强,其次是 H1N1 和 H7N9。在体内,Gal3 缺陷小鼠的存活率显着提高,体重减轻减少,肺部炎症减轻,感染后 IL-1β 释放减少,对 H5N1 的保护作用最明显。
解释与创新
研究结果确定 Gal3 是一个关键的 宿主 因子,它同时促进病毒进入并驱动 NLRP3 炎性体组装,从而在甲型流感病毒感染期间协调过度炎症。这种双阶段机制凸显了 Gal3-NLRP3 轴作为减轻流感引起的严重肺损伤的新型治疗靶点。
科研与公卫意义
该研究表明,针对 Gal3-NLRP3 轴可以通过限制病毒进入和过度炎症反应来减轻严重流感病例的疾病严重程度,特别是由 H5N1 等高致病性毒株引起的病例。
局限与证据强度
证据仅来自摘要,这限制了详细的方法和结果报告,可能会省略关键控制、样本量和统计分析。因此,证据的强度是中等的,有待全面的方法和结果验证。

Galectin-3 exacerbates influenza a virus pathogenesis via dual mechanisms: Promoting hemagglutinin-dependent entry and driving nlrp3 inflammasome activation.

Authors: Zih-Syuan Yang, Arunee Thitithanyanont, Jason C. Huang, Yen-Hsu Chen, Fu-Tong Liu, Wanchai Assavalapsakul, Sheng-Fan Wang
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A viruses (IAVs), particularly highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) strains like H5N1 and H7N9, cause severe pneumonia and acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) driven by dysregulated cytokine storms. A key mediator of this immunopathology is the excessive activation of the NLRP3 inflammasome, which governs the maturation of IL-1β. Galectin-3 (Gal3), a β-galactoside-binding lectin, is upregulated during infection; however, its specific regulatory role in coordinating NLRP3 inflammasome activation across distinct viral hemagglutinin (HA) subtypes remains to be elucidated. In this study, we demonstrate that Gal3 functions as a critical amplifier of influenza pathogenesis through a distinct dual-stage mechanism. Results from viral binding and internalization assays indicate that extracellular Gal3 promotes viral entry by interacting with viral HA, an effect that is strictly strain-dependent (H5N1 > H1N1 > H7N9). This enhanced entry consequently amplifies the initial signal for inflammasome activation. Intracellularly, Gal3 regulates the NLRP3 cascade by sustaining NF-κB activation (priming) to upregulate NLRP3 and pro-IL-1β transcription and acting as a facilitator to strengthen the NLRP3-ASC-pro-caspase-1 interaction, promoting ASC oligomerization (activation). Consistently, in vivo studies demonstrate that Gal3-deficient mice exhibit significantly improved survival, reduced weight loss, and attenuated pulmonary inflammation and IL-1β release following infection, with the most pronounced protective effects observed against H5N1. Collectively, these findings identify Gal3 as a potent host factor that orchestrates excessive inflammation by simultaneously facilitating viral entry and driving the assembly of the NLRP3 inflammasome, highlighting the Gal3-NLRP3 axis as a promising therapeutic target to mitigate severe influenza-induced lung injury.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses (IAVs), including highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) strains such as H5N1 and H7N9, cause severe respiratory disease via dysregulated immune responses, particularly cytokine storms driven by excessive NLRP3 inflammasome activation. The study aimed to elucidate the regulatory role of galectin-3 (Gal3) in coordinating NLRP3 inflammasome activation across distinct viral hemagglutinin (HA) subtypes.
Design and population
The study investigated the dual mechanisms by which Gal3 exacerbates influenza A virus pathogenesis using in vitro viral binding and internalization assays, as well as in vivo infection models in mice. The research focused on the interaction between Gal3 and viral HA subtypes (H5N1, H1N1, H7N9) and its downstream effects on inflammasome activation.
Core methods
Viral binding and internalization assays were performed to assess the effect of extracellular Gal3 on viral entry across different HA subtypes. Intracellular mechanisms were examined by evaluating Gal3's role in sustaining NF-κB activation to upregulate NLRP3 and pro-IL-1β transcription, as well as facilitating the NLRP3-ASC-pro-caspase-1 interaction and ASC oligomerization.
Main results
Extracellular Gal3 promoted viral entry in a strain-dependent manner, with the strongest effect observed for H5N1, followed by H1N1 and H7N9. In vivo, Gal3-deficient mice exhibited significantly improved survival, reduced weight loss, attenuated pulmonary inflammation, and decreased IL-1β release following infection, with the most pronounced protective effects against H5N1.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings identify Gal3 as a critical host factor that simultaneously facilitates viral entry and drives NLRP3 inflammasome assembly, thereby orchestrating excessive inflammation during influenza A virus infection. This dual-stage mechanism highlights the Gal3-NLRP3 axis as a novel therapeutic target to mitigate severe influenza-induced lung injury.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study suggests that targeting the Gal3-NLRP3 axis could reduce disease severity in severe influenza cases, particularly those caused by highly pathogenic strains like H5N1, by limiting both viral entry and excessive inflammatory responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is derived solely from the abstract, which limits detailed methodological and result reporting, potentially omitting key controls, sample sizes, and statistical analyses. The strength of the evidence is therefore moderate, pending full methodological and result validation.

鸟类迁徙过程中 高致病性禽流感 传播的决定因素

Determinants of highly pathogenic avian influenza spread during bird migration
作者： Camille M. Montalcini, Bert Wuyts, Thijs Kuiken, Silke Bauer
摘要中文翻译摘要 迁徙水禽是 H5 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 鹅/广东谱系在全球传播的核心,但影响其作用的参数和过程仍然知之甚少,证据分散在集中于狭窄参数组的研究中。在这里,我们综合了当前的知识来确定 43 个参数,并开发了一个基于空间显式代理的水禽迁徙模型,以评估其中 28 个参数对 9 个 HPAI 结果的影响。我们的研究结果表明,在鸟与鸟之间 传播 率高、传染期长以及鸟类在中途停留地聚集较多的情况下,迁徙水禽对 HPAI 传播和风险贡献最大。当聚集水平保持不变时,个体之间迁徙路线的较低重叠(“空间对齐”)通过延长水禽种群和环境中的感染维持来进一步放大水禽的贡献。这些结果表明,一些病毒参数在很大程度上影响了感染动态,而且通过聚集和空间排列的迁移行为在我们的模型中发挥着相对重要的作用。病毒特征强烈影响水禽中的感染 患病率 和家禽的入侵风险,而迁徙行为更强烈地影响 暴发 的时间、维持和空间分布。这些发现强调了将水禽运动生态学纳入高致病性禽流感风险模型和评估的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解鸟类迁徙过程中影响 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 传播的因素,因为迁徙水禽是其全球传播的核心,但影响其作用的参数和过程仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究综合了当前的知识,确定了 43 个参数,并开发了一个基于空间显式代理的水禽迁徙模型,以评估 28 个参数对 9 个 HPAI 结果的影响。
核心方法
研究人员使用基于空间显式代理的水禽迁徙模型来评估 28 个参数对 9 个 HPAI 结果的影响,综合当前知识以确定 43 个参数。
主要结果
结果表明,在禽类 传播 率高、传染期长、中途停留地鸟类聚集较多的情况下,迁徙水禽对 HPAI 传播和风险贡献最大。当聚集水平保持不变时,个体之间迁徙路线的较低重叠(“空间对齐”)会通过延长水禽种群和环境中的感染维持来进一步放大水禽的贡献。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,一些病毒参数在很大程度上影响了感染动态,而且通过聚集和空间排列的迁移行为在模型中发挥着相对重要的作用。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了将水禽运动生态学纳入 HPAI 风险模型和评估以更好地预测和管理 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于当前知识和模型的抽象综合,这可能会限制研究结果的可信度,但由于综合参数评估,证据的强度被认为是中等的。

Determinants of highly pathogenic avian influenza spread during bird migration

Authors: Camille M. Montalcini, Bert Wuyts, Thijs Kuiken, Silke Bauer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Migratory waterfowl are central to the global spread of the Goose/Guangdong lineage of H5 highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI), yet the parameters and processes shaping their role remain poorly understood, with evidence scattered across studies focused on narrow sets of parameters. Here, we synthesized current knowledge to identify 43 parameters and developed a spatially explicit agent-based model of waterfowl migration to evaluate the influence of 28 of these parameters on nine HPAI outcomes. Our results show that migratory waterfowl contribute most to HPAI spread and risk under conditions of high bird-to-bird transmission rate, long infectious periods and greater aggregation of birds at stop-over sites. When aggregation level is held constant, lower overlap in migratory routes among individuals (‘spatial alignment’) further amplifies waterfowl contributions by extending infection maintenance in waterfowl population and the environment. These results suggest that a few viral parameters shape much of infection dynamics, but also that migration behaviour, via aggregation and spatial alignment, plays a comparably large role within our model. Viral traits strongly shaped infection prevalence in waterfowl and incursion risk to poultry, whereas migratory behaviour more strongly shaped outbreak timing, maintenance and spatial distribution. These findings underscore the importance of integrating waterfowl movement ecology into HPAI risk models and assessments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the factors influencing the spread of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) during bird migration, as migratory waterfowl are central to its global spread but the parameters and processes shaping their role remain poorly understood.
Design and population
The study synthesized current knowledge to identify 43 parameters and developed a spatially explicit agent-based model of waterfowl migration to evaluate the influence of 28 parameters on nine HPAI outcomes.
Core methods
The researchers used a spatially explicit agent-based model of waterfowl migration to evaluate the influence of 28 parameters on nine HPAI outcomes, synthesizing current knowledge to identify 43 parameters.
Main results
The results showed that migratory waterfowl contribute most to HPAI spread and risk under conditions of high bird-to-bird transmission rate, long infectious periods, and greater aggregation of birds at stop-over sites. When aggregation level is held constant, lower overlap in migratory routes among individuals (‘spatial alignment’) further amplifies waterfowl contributions by extending infection maintenance in the waterfowl population and the environment.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that a few viral parameters shape much of infection dynamics, but also that migration behaviour, via aggregation and spatial alignment, plays a comparably large role within the model.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the importance of integrating waterfowl movement ecology into HPAI risk models and assessments to better predict and manage outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an abstract-only synthesis of current knowledge and a model, which may limit the confidence in the findings, but the evidence is considered moderate in strength due to the comprehensive parameter evaluation.

摩洛哥家禽业的流动网络分析:监测 和预防 高致病性禽流感 的工具

Mobility Network Analysis of the Poultry Sector in Morocco: A Tool for the Surveillance and Prevention of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza
作者： Fadoua Boudouma, Yahya Farhi, Mohammed Dehhaoui, Hicham Hajji, Oumayma Arbani, Kenza Aitelkadi, Siham Fellahi
摘要中文翻译背景:高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 对全球家禽业和公共卫生构成严重威胁。尽管摩洛哥目前保持着无高致病性禽流感的状态,但由于其位于主要迁徙路线沿线以及家禽行业内广泛的商业贸易网络,该国面临着巨大的风险。现有数据表明,活禽市场、生产农场和家禽部门相关设施之间的联系构成了疾病 流行病学 的关键决定因素。本研究旨在分析这些运动并确定它们如何影响疾病的传播,并指导政策制定者制定有效的基于风险的 监测 和适合当地情况的控制策略。:在卡萨布兰卡-塞塔特地区(包括 9 个省和 138 个直辖市)进行了一项基于问卷的横断面调查,以调查该地区家禽业的运动模式。采用社交网络分析 (SNA) 构建移动网络,并使用地理信息系统 (GIS) 对结果进行空间可视化,并通过 R. 中的网络分析进行分析: 126 个城市总共记录了 945 条交通路线。中心性度量确定了表现出高程度和介数中心性值的三个主要网络节点。我们的研究结果表明,尽管网络密度较低,但由于频繁的双向链路和异构网络结构,IAHP 的 传播 仍然至关重要。一些“中心”城市的活动高度集中,导致早期出现和快速传播。:确定具有高度影响力的节点使兽医当局能够优先考虑将 监测 活动瞄准最有可能引入疾病或持续存在的城市。该网络的结构连通性表明理论上存在 HPAI 快速传播的潜力,强调了城市之间频繁双向联系的重要性。这保护了摩洛哥的家禽业和公众健康。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 威胁着全球家禽业和公共卫生,而摩洛哥由于其沿迁徙路线和广泛的家禽贸易网络的地位,面临着更大的风险。该研究解决了家禽运动模式如何影响高致病性禽流感传播的知识差距,为有针对性的 监测 和控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
在摩洛哥卡萨布兰卡-塞塔特地区进行了一项基于问卷调查的横断面调查,覆盖 9 个省和 138 个市,以绘制家禽流动模式图。该研究记录了 126 个城市的 945 条交通路线,并使用社交网络分析 (SNA) 和 GIS 可视化来表征网络结构。
核心方法
采用社交网络分析(SNA)构建运动网络,并使用地理信息系统(GIS)进行空间可视化。在 R 中进行网络分析,并应用中心性度量来识别家禽运输网络中的关键节点。
主要结果
三个主要网络节点被确定为具有高度数和介数中心性值。该网络表现出低密度,但由于频繁的双向链接和异构结构而保持了关键的 传播 潜力。
解释与创新
该研究强调,少数高度互联的城市充当枢纽,使疾病能够尽早出现并迅速传播。这种基于网络的方法提供了一种新颖的工具,用于优先考虑 监测 和高风险领域的控制工作。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现使兽医当局能够针对高致病性禽流感传入或持续存在最大风险的城市采取 监测 和干预措施,从而保护摩洛哥的家禽业和公共卫生。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于卡萨布兰卡-塞塔特地区,并依赖于基于问卷的数据,该数据可能无法全面捕获所有的运动模式。由于横断面设计和缺乏直接疾病 传播 数据,证据强度中等。

Mobility Network Analysis of the Poultry Sector in Morocco: A Tool for the Surveillance and Prevention of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza

Authors: Fadoua Boudouma, Yahya Farhi, Mohammed Dehhaoui, Hicham Hajji, Oumayma Arbani, Kenza Aitelkadi, Siham Fellahi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) poses a critical global threat to both the poultry industry and public health. Although Morocco currently maintains HPAI-free status, the country faces substantial risk due to its location along major migratory flyways and its extensive commercial trade networks within the poultry sector. Available data indicate that the links between live bird markets, production farms, and related facilities in the poultry sector constitute a pivotal determinant of disease epidemiology. This study aimed to analyze these movements and determine how they can influence the spread of the disease and to guide policymakers in developing effective risk-based surveillance and control strategies tailored to the local context.: A questionnaire-based cross-sectional survey was conducted across the Casablanca-Settat region, including nine provinces and 138 municipalities, to investigate the movement patterns within the poultry sector in this region. Social network analysis (SNA) was employed to construct a movement network, and findings were spatially visualized using Geographic Information Systems (GIS) and analyzed through network analysis in R.: A total of 945 transport routes were recorded in 126 municipalities. Centrality measures identified three predominant network nodes exhibiting high degree and betweenness centrality values. Our findings indicate that although the network has a low density, the transmission of IAHP remains critical due to frequent bidirectional links and a heterogeneous network structure. The high concentration of movements by a few “hub” municipalities leads to an early emergence and rapid dissemination.: The identification of highly influential nodes allows veterinary authorities to prioritize and target surveillance activities toward municipalities with the greatest likelihood of disease introduction or persistence. The network’s structural connectivity suggests a theoretical potential for rapid HPAI spread, underscoring the importance of the frequent bidirectional links identified between municipalities. This protects both Morocco’s poultry industry and public health.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) threatens poultry industries and public health globally, and Morocco faces heightened risk due to its position along migratory flyways and extensive poultry trade networks. The study addressed a knowledge gap on how poultry movement patterns influence HPAI spread to inform targeted surveillance and control strategies.
Design and population
A questionnaire-based cross-sectional survey was conducted in the Casablanca-Settat region of Morocco, covering nine provinces and 138 municipalities to map poultry movement patterns. The study recorded 945 transport routes across 126 municipalities and used social network analysis (SNA) with GIS visualization to characterize the network structure.
Core methods
Social network analysis (SNA) was employed to construct a movement network, and Geographic Information Systems (GIS) were used for spatial visualization. Network analysis was performed in R, with centrality measures applied to identify key nodes in the poultry movement network.
Main results
Three predominant network nodes were identified with high degree and betweenness centrality values. The network exhibited low density but maintained critical transmission potential due to frequent bidirectional links and a heterogeneous structure.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that a small number of highly connected municipalities act as hubs, enabling early disease emergence and rapid dissemination. This network-based approach provides a novel tool for prioritizing surveillance and control efforts in high-risk areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings enable veterinary authorities to target surveillance and interventions toward municipalities with the greatest risk of HPAI introduction or persistence, thereby protecting Morocco’s poultry industry and public health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to the Casablanca-Settat region and relies on questionnaire-based data, which may not capture all movement patterns comprehensively. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and absence of direct disease transmission data.

Educação sanitária como estratégia de vigilância sanitária: relato de uma Campanha Extensionista sobre Influenza aviária 作为卫生 监测 战略的卫生教育:关于 禽流感 推广活动的报告严重流行性感冒

Educação sanitária como estratégia de vigilância sanitária: relato de uma campanha extensionista sobre influenza aviária Sanitary education as a sanitary surveillance strategy: report of an extension campaign on avian influenza Educación sanitaria como estrategia de vigilancia sanitaria: relato de una campaña de extensión sobre influenza aviar
作者： Leandro dos Santos Machado, Pamella Pryscila de Alvarenga Bissoli Maciel de Lima, Maria Rita Dager Costa Vieira, Roberto Teixeira de Oliveira, Fernanda Barcelos Amaral, Danilo Muniz Aragão, Milena da Fonseca Costa, Jessyca Kelly Ferreira Costa, Huarrisson Azevedo Santos, Túlio Leite Reis
摘要中文翻译流感禽鸟 (IA) 是巴西禽业卫生的重要组成部分,它不影响世界各地的环境。一份关于屠宰场的报告代表了动物卫生、公共事业和生物多样性的风险,以及对经济协会和限制卫生和商业的影响的潜力。就其背景而言,卫生教育的目的是为了大学的扩展,并承担传播技术信息和社会责任的战略。我们提出了关于流感禽流感的客观描述和教育分析,以及与大学的扩展,以及在大学和社会中的科学发展潜力。为实现 2025 年的目标,我们将在全国科学与技术周 (SNCT-2025) 期间开展四小时活动,致力于材料教学的精心设计和社区指导教育活动的发展acadêmica,一家农村和公众生产商。讨论病因学、传播形式、西奈临床、生物安全的医疗以及治疗前通知的重要性。Ação alcançou 50 参与者可能会转移相关技术以获取信息,并为扩大信息和卫生意识做出贡献。其结果证明了推广的潜力,可以有效预防集体暴力,促进资格信息、社会参与和卫生保卫工作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 禽流感 带来的公共卫生和动物健康挑战,该挑战对巴西家禽业和全球健康构成重大威胁。
设计与对象
该研究描述了 2025 年 10 月国家科技周 (SNCT-2025) 期间开展的一项推广活动,目标包括学术界成员、农村生产者和当地居民在内的 50 名参与者。
核心方法
该活动涉及开发教育材料和活动,旨在传播有关 禽流感 的技术信息,包括其病因学、传播、临床症状、生物安全措施以及早期疾病通知的重要性。
主要结果
该活动成功吸引了 50 名参与者,促进了技术知识的便捷传播,提高了目标群体的健康意识和对 禽流感 的理解水平。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明了推广活动在提高公众健康意识和加强卫生 监测 方面的有效性,强调了通过社区参与预防疾病的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在 禽流感 监测 的背景下,推广活动可以在改善公共卫生准备和促进学术机构与当地社区之间的合作方面发挥至关重要的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要报告,限制了分析的深度。然而,所提供的证据支持对推广活动作为一项公共卫生战略的有效性有一定的信心。

Educação sanitária como estratégia de vigilância sanitária: relato de uma campanha extensionista sobre influenza aviária Sanitary education as a sanitary surveillance strategy: report of an extension campaign on avian influenza Educación sanitaria como estrategia de vigilancia sanitaria: relato de una campaña de extensión sobre influenza aviar

Authors: Leandro dos Santos Machado, Pamella Pryscila de Alvarenga Bissoli Maciel de Lima, Maria Rita Dager Costa Vieira, Roberto Teixeira de Oliveira, Fernanda Barcelos Amaral, Danilo Muniz Aragão, Milena da Fonseca Costa, Jessyca Kelly Ferreira Costa, Huarrisson Azevedo Santos, Túlio Leite Reis
Original abstract (English)
A Influenza Aviária (IA) constitui um importante desafio sanitário para a avicultura brasileira, setor no qual o país ocupa posição de destaque no cenário mundial. A recorrência de casos da enfermidade representa risco à sanidade animal, à saúde pública e à biodiversidade, além de potenciais impactos econômicos associados a restrições sanitárias e comerciais. Nesse contexto, ações de educação sanitária desenvolvidas no âmbito da extensão universitária assumem papel estratégico na disseminação de informações técnicas e na conscientização da sociedade. O presente trabalho teve como objetivo descrever e analisar uma campanha de educação em saúde sanitária sobre Influenza Aviária, desenvolvida como ação extensionista universitária, avaliando seu potencial como estratégia de conscientização e aproximação entre universidade e sociedade. A atividade foi realizada em outubro de 2025, com carga horária de quatro horas, durante a Semana Nacional de Ciência e Tecnologia (SNCT-2025), e envolveu a elaboração de materiais didáticos e o desenvolvimento de atividades educativas direcionadas à comunidade acadêmica, a produtores rurais e ao público do entorno. Foram abordados aspectos relacionados à etiologia, às formas de transmissão, aos sinais clínicos, às medidas de biosseguridade e à importância da notificação precoce da doença. A ação alcançou 50 participantes e possibilitou a transferência de conhecimentos técnicos de forma acessível, contribuindo para a ampliação do nível de informação e da conscientização sanitária. Os resultados evidenciam o potencial das ações extensionistas como ferramentas eficazes na prevenção de enfermidades de impacto coletivo, ao promoverem informação qualificada, participação social e fortalecimento da vigilância sanitária.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the public health and animal health challenges posed by avian influenza, which is a significant threat to Brazil's poultry industry and global health.
Design and population
The study describes an extension campaign conducted in October 2025 during the National Science and Technology Week (SNCT-2025), targeting 50 participants including academic community members, rural producers, and local residents.
Core methods
The campaign involved the development of educational materials and activities designed to disseminate technical information about avian influenza, including its etiology, transmission, clinical signs, biosafety measures, and the importance of early disease notification.
Main results
The campaign successfully reached 50 participants and facilitated the transfer of technical knowledge in an accessible manner, enhancing the level of health awareness and understanding of avian influenza among the target groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of extension activities in promoting public health awareness and strengthening sanitary surveillance, highlighting a novel approach to disease prevention through community engagement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that extension campaigns can play a crucial role in improving public health preparedness and fostering collaboration between academic institutions and local communities in the context of avian influenza surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only report, which restricts the depth of analysis. However, the evidence provided supports a moderate confidence in the effectiveness of the extension campaign as a public health strategy.

生态因素和 宿主 因素之间的复杂相互作用驱动欧亚大陆和非洲 禽流感 暴发 风险的大陆模式

Complex interactions between ecological and host factors drive continental patterns of avian influenza outbreak risk in Eurasia and Africa
作者： Moh A. Alkhamis, Nicholas M. Fountain-Jones, Irene Iglesias, Abrar Husain, Shahad Alhay, Andres M. Perez
摘要中文翻译本研究采用多算法机器学习统计框架,并基于27个生态学特征,分析了2004年至2025年间在欧亚大陆和非洲报告的超过50,000例禽流感病例(暴发)。数据被分层为高致病性禽流感(HPAI)、低致病性禽流感(LPAI)、家禽和野生鸟类子集。摘要中禽流感病毒(AIV)具有通过快速突变和跨物种传播进行适应的显著能力,构成了重大全球大流行威胁。禽流感病例(暴发)的不同空间分布模式表明,疾病出现与生态驱动因素之间的关系复杂、非线性,并随病毒致病性和宿主特征而变化。我们的模型实现了高预测性能(准确率≥84%),并揭示了由强非线性相互作用驱动的、特定分层的风险特征。对于所有禽流感病例(队列研究)和野生鸟类子集,植被指数和湿地邻近度是空间风险的最重要预测因子。相反,家鸭和鸡的密度在LPAI和家禽子系统的预测中占主导地位,而LPAI禽流感病例(暴发)的邻近度则是HPAI风险的最强驱动因素。尽管野生动物和发展中地区的漏报限制了数据质量,但该可解释框架为支持基于风险的监测(监测)提供了灵活平台,并可在区域和全球尺度上优化有针对性的干预措施。资助 本手稿部分由美国农业部农业研究局(USDA:ARS)授予明尼苏达大学动物健康与食品安全中心的资助资助。伦理声明 利益冲突 作者声明无利益冲突。补充信息 出版商注 Springer Nature 在此方面保持中立。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解生态因素和 宿主 因素如何相互作用,从而驱动欧亚大陆和非洲 禽流感 暴发 风险的大陆模式,强调疾病出现与这些因素之间复杂且非线性的关系。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2004 年至 2025 年欧亚大陆和非洲报告的 50,000 多个 暴发,将数据分为高致病性 (HPAI)、低致病性 (LPAI)、家养鸟类和野生鸟类子集。
核心方法
使用多算法机器学习统计框架,结合 27 个生态特征来对不同阶层的 禽流感 暴发 的风险因素进行建模。
主要结果
该模型实现了高预测性能,准确度≥ 84%,揭示了由生态因素和 宿主 因素之间强烈的非线性相互作用驱动的独特的、特定阶层的风险状况。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,植被指数和湿地邻近度是全AIV和野鸟队列研究的关键预测因素,而家鸭和鸡的密度主导着LPAI和家禽子系统的预测,而与HPAI 暴发的邻近度是HPAI风险的最强驱动因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为基于风险的 监测 和有针对性的干预措施提供了灵活的框架,可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,以在区域和全球范围内减轻 禽流感 暴发 的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认野生动物和发展中地区的漏报是一个限制,这可能会影响数据质量,但模型的高预测准确性支持对研究结果的适度信心。

Complex interactions between ecological and host factors drive continental patterns of avian influenza outbreak risk in Eurasia and Africa

Authors: Moh A. Alkhamis, Nicholas M. Fountain-Jones, Irene Iglesias, Abrar Husain, Shahad Alhay, Andres M. Perez
Original abstract (English)
Here, we used a multi-algorithm machine learning statistical framework and 27 ecological features to analyze over 50,000 outbreaks reported across Eurasia and Africa from 2004 to 2025, stratifying the data into highly pathogenic (HPAI), low-pathogenic (LPAI), domestic, and wild-bird subsets. Abstract Avian influenza viruses (AIV) have a remarkable capacity to adapt through rapid mutation and cross-species transmission, posing a major global pandemic threat. The distinct spatial patterns of outbreaks suggest that the relationship between disease emergence and ecological drivers is complex, non-linear, and varies with virus pathogenicity and host characteristics. Our models achieved high predictive performance (accuracies ≥ 84%) and revealed distinct, strata-specific risk profiles driven by strong non-linear interactions. For the all-AIV and wild-bird cohorts, vegetation indices and wetland proximity were the most important predictors of spatial risk. Conversely, domestic duck and chicken densities dominated predictions for the LPAI and domestic poultry subsystems, while proximity to LPAI outbreaks was the strongest driver of HPAI risk. Although underreporting in wildlife and developing regions limits data quality, this interpretable framework provides a flexible platform to support risk-based surveillance and to optimize targeted interventions at regional and global scales. Funding This manuscript was funded in part by a grant from the United States Department of Agriculture Agriculture Research Service (USDA:ARS) awarded to the University of Minnesota Center for Animal Health and Food Safety. Ethics declarations Competing interests The authors declare no competing interests. Additional information Publisher’s note Springer Nature remains neutral with regard to jurisdic...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how ecological and host factors interact to drive continental patterns of avian influenza outbreak risk in Eurasia and Africa, highlighting the complex and non-linear relationship between disease emergence and these factors.
Design and population
The study analyzed over 50,000 outbreaks reported across Eurasia and Africa from 2004 to 2025, stratifying the data into highly pathogenic (HPAI), low-pathogenic (LPAI), domestic, and wild-bird subsets.
Core methods
A multi-algorithm machine learning statistical framework was used, incorporating 27 ecological features to model the risk factors for avian influenza outbreaks across different strata.
Main results
The models achieved high predictive performance with accuracies ≥ 84%, revealing distinct, strata-specific risk profiles driven by strong non-linear interactions between ecological and host factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that vegetation indices and wetland proximity are key predictors for all-AIV and wild-bird cohorts, while domestic duck and chicken densities dominate predictions for the LPAI and domestic poultry subsystems, and proximity to HPAI outbreaks is the strongest driver for HPAI risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a flexible framework for risk-based surveillance and targeted interventions, which can inform public health strategies to mitigate avian influenza outbreaks at regional and global scales.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges underreporting in wildlife and developing regions as a limitation, which may affect data quality, but the high predictive accuracy of the models supports a moderate confidence in the findings.

HPAI 后塘鹅抗体水平高度持久,但群体免疫力有限 暴发

Highly persistent antibody levels but limited population immunity in gannets after HPAI outbreak
作者： Thierry Boulinier, Mathilde Lejeune, Pascale Massin, Niqueux E, Armel Deniau, Woerle R, Alice Bernard, Aurore Ponchon, T.J. Martin, Jérôme Fort
摘要中文翻译最近,进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的高致病性 禽流感 (HP AI) 病毒 H5Nx 的大规模传播导致野生物种大规模死亡,特别是长寿海鸟,对幸存个体的免疫力影响未知。在北大西洋,北部塘鹅种群在 2022 年受到严重影响,在繁殖中的成体中观察到超过 40% 的 死亡率 和一些幸存个体出现深色虹膜。使用 2023 年和 2024 年在 Rouzic 群体(法国)收集的样本,我们报告了持续的个体抗 AI 抗体水平和血清中和滴度,其中大多数免疫个体呈现深色虹膜。建模方法进一步强调了此类物种中持久免疫力的重要性,该方法表明,在重复 暴发 的情况下,多年来保持免疫力的个体比例受到强烈限制,种群规模会减少。总的来说,我们的结果强调了长寿物种中持久免疫力的存在及其对于种群持续存在的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查最近 HPAI H5Nx 病毒的大规模传播对幸存者的免疫力的影响,特别是大规模死亡后的长寿海鸟。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注北大西洋的北部塘鹅群体,特别是法国的 Rouzic 群体,于 2023 年和 2024 年收集了样本,以评估幸存个体的抗体水平和血清中和滴度。
核心方法
使用 2023 年和 2024 年从 Rouzic 群体收集的样本测量了抗体水平和血清中和滴度。采用建模方法来评估重复 暴发 期间持久免疫力对群体持续性的影响。
主要结果
观察到持续的个体抗 AI 抗体水平和血清中和滴度,大多数免疫个体呈现深色虹膜。建模方法表明,在重复的 暴发 期间,显着限制种群规模的年份之间保持免疫力的个体比例下降。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,持久的免疫力在长寿物种的种群持续存在中发挥着至关重要的作用,强调了免疫力在减轻反复 HPAI 暴发 影响方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对长寿海鸟免疫动态的深入了解,这可以为 监测 和 禽流感 暴发 的保护策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于从单个菌落收集的样本,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,由于对持久性抗体水平的直接观察以及使用建模方法来支持解释,证据是强有力的。

Highly persistent antibody levels but limited population immunity in gannets after HPAI outbreak

Authors: Thierry Boulinier, Mathilde Lejeune, Pascale Massin, Niqueux E, Armel Deniau, Woerle R, Alice Bernard, Aurore Ponchon, T.J. Martin, Jérôme Fort
Original abstract (English)
The recent large-scale circulation of High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza (HP AI) viruses H5Nx of clade 2.3.4.4b has been responsible for massive die-offs in wild species, notably in long-lived seabirds, with unknown implications for the immunity of surviving individuals. In the North Atlantic, northern gannet colonies were heavily affected in 2022, with more than 40% mortality observed among breeding adults and some surviving individuals developing dark irises. Using samples collected in 2023 and 2024 on Rouzic colony (France), we report persistent individual anti-AI antibody levels and seroneutralisation titres, with most of the immune individuals showing dark irises. A modelling approach further stressed the importance of long-lasting immunity in such species by showing that the proportion of individuals which kept their immunity between years strongly limited decreases in population size in case of repeated outbreaks. Overall, our results highlight the existence and importance of long-lasting immunity in long-lived species for population persistence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the implications of the recent large-scale circulation of HPAI H5Nx viruses on the immunity of surviving individuals, particularly in long-lived seabirds, following massive die-offs.
Design and population
The study focused on northern gannet colonies in the North Atlantic, specifically the Rouzic colony in France, where samples were collected in 2023 and 2024 to assess antibody levels and seroneutralisation titres in surviving individuals.
Core methods
Antibody levels and seroneutralisation titres were measured using samples collected from the Rouzic colony in 2023 and 2024. A modelling approach was employed to evaluate the impact of long-lasting immunity on population persistence during repeated outbreaks.
Main results
Persistent individual anti-AI antibody levels and seroneutralisation titres were observed, with most immune individuals showing dark irises. The modelling approach indicated that the proportion of individuals maintaining immunity between years significantly limited population size decreases during repeated outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that long-lasting immunity plays a crucial role in population persistence for long-lived species, highlighting the importance of immunity in mitigating the impact of repeated HPAI outbreaks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the dynamics of immunity in long-lived seabirds, which can inform surveillance and conservation strategies for avian influenza outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on samples collected from a single colony, which may limit generalisability. However, the evidence is strong due to the direct observation of persistent antibody levels and the use of a modelling approach to support the interpretation.

基于MIRA-PfAgo的天布苏病毒快速检测方法的建立

Establishment of a rapid detection method for Tembusu virus based on MIRA-PfAgo.
作者： Bei Wang, Liwei Liu, Xiaoqing Wang, Kai Yang, Daojun Chen, Dongdong Yin
摘要中文翻译本研究开发了一种基于多酶等温快速扩增(MIRA)结合激烈火球菌Argonaute(PfAgo)裂解系统(MIRA-PfAgo)的新型核酸检测方法,用于检测威胁水禽业的重要病原体天布苏病毒(TMUV)。该方法针对 TMUV 高度保守的 NS5 基因。目标核酸在 39°C 等温条件下通过 MIRA 快速富集,然后在序列特异性引导 DNA (gDNA) 的引导下进行 PfAgo 介导的切割,从而能够通过荧光信号输出进行视觉检测。系统优化确定 0.5 μM gDNA、1 μM PfAgo 和 5 mM Mn2+ 为最佳反应条件。该检测对TMUV表现出优异的特异性,与常见水禽病原体没有观察到交叉反应,包括H9亚型禽流感病毒(AIV-H9)、鸭肠炎病毒(DEV)、鹅细小病毒(GPV)、番鸭呼肠孤病毒(MDRV)、新城疫病毒(NDV)和鸭圆环病毒(DuCV)。灵敏度为 100 拷贝/μl。在对21个临床样本的分析中,MIRA-PfAgo检测结果与RT-PCR获得的结果完全一致。该方法不需要复杂的热循环设备,灵敏度高、特异性强、操作简单,为TMUV的快速现场监测提供了可靠实用的技术工具。研究亮点这是首次利用MIRA-PfAgo检测法检测TMUV。结果可通过肉眼观察到。所开发的检测法有望为TMUV的检测提供一种替代手段,所需设备最少。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在利用基于 MIRA-PfAgo 的新型核酸检测方法,开发一种威胁水禽业的重要病原体天布苏病毒(TMUV)的快速检测方法。
设计与对象
针对 TMUV 高度保守的 NS5 基因,开发了一种新的检测方法。该研究涉及反应条件的系统优化和 21 个临床样本的分析以验证该方法。
核心方法
该方法采用多酶等温快速扩增(MIRA)在39°C等温条件下快速富集目标核酸,然后在序列特异性引导DNA(gDNA)引导下进行PfAgo介导的切割,通过荧光信号输出实现视觉检测。使用 0.5 μM gDNA、1 μM PfAgo 和 5 mM Mn2+ 作为最佳反应条件对测定进行了优化。
主要结果
MIRA-PfAgo 检测对 TMUV 显示出极好的特异性,与常见水禽病原体没有观察到交叉反应。该方法的灵敏度为100拷贝/μl,与RT-PCR分析21个临床样本时得到的结果完全一致。
解释与创新
MIRA-PfAgo 测定为 TMUV 的快速现场监测提供了可靠且实用的技术工具,提供了一种需要最少设备且更适合现场应用的替代检测方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为快速、灵敏地检测 TMUV 提供了一种新方法,可以增强 监测 和水禽行业的控制工作,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于相对较少的临床样本。然而,该方法已针对 RT-PCR 进行了验证,并且证据支持其可靠性和实用性具有中等可信度。

Establishment of a rapid detection method for Tembusu virus based on MIRA-PfAgo.

Authors: Bei Wang, Liwei Liu, Xiaoqing Wang, Kai Yang, Daojun Chen, Dongdong Yin
Original abstract (English)
In this study, a novel nucleic acid detection method based on multienzyme isothermal rapid amplification (MIRA) combined with the Pyrococcus furiosus Argonaute (PfAgo) cleavage system (MIRA-PfAgo) was developed for the detection of Tembusu virus (TMUV), an important pathogen threatening the waterfowl industry. The method targets the highly conserved NS5 gene of TMUV. Target nucleic acids are rapidly enriched by MIRA under isothermal conditions at 39°C, followed by PfAgo-mediated cleavage guided by sequence-specific guide DNA (gDNA), enabling visual detection through fluorescence signal output. Systematic optimization identified 0.5 μM gDNA, 1 μM PfAgo, and 5 mM Mn2+ as the optimal reaction conditions. The assay exhibited excellent specificity for TMUV, with no cross-reactivity observed with common waterfowl pathogens, including H9 subtype avian influenza virus (AIV-H9), duck enteritis virus (DEV), goose parvovirus (GPV), Muscovy duck reovirus (MDRV), Newcastle disease virus (NDV), and duck circovirus (DuCV). The sensitivity was 100 copies/μl. In the analysis of 21 clinical samples, the results of the MIRA-PfAgo assay were fully consistent with those obtained by RT-PCR. This method does not require complex thermal cycling equipment and combines high sensitivity, strong specificity, and operational simplicity, providing a reliable and practical technical tool for rapid on-site monitoring of TMUV.RESEARCH HIGHLIGHTSThis is the first study to utilize the MIRA-PfAgo detection assay for the detection of TMUV.The results are observable by the unaided eye.The developed assay potentially offers an alternative means for detection of TMUV, requiring minimal equipment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a rapid detection method for Tembusu virus (TMUV), an important pathogen threatening the waterfowl industry, by utilizing a novel nucleic acid detection approach based on MIRA-PfAgo.
Design and population
A novel detection method was developed for TMUV, targeting its highly conserved NS5 gene. The study involved systematic optimization of reaction conditions and analysis of 21 clinical samples to validate the method.
Core methods
The method employs multienzyme isothermal rapid amplification (MIRA) under isothermal conditions at 39°C to rapidly enrich target nucleic acids, followed by PfAgo-mediated cleavage guided by sequence-specific guide DNA (gDNA), enabling visual detection through fluorescence signal output. The assay was optimized with 0.5 μM gDNA, 1 μM PfAgo, and 5 mM Mn2+ as the optimal reaction conditions.
Main results
The MIRA-PfAgo assay showed excellent specificity for TMUV, with no cross-reactivity observed with common waterfowl pathogens. The sensitivity of the assay was 100 copies/μl, and the results were fully consistent with those obtained by RT-PCR when analyzing 21 clinical samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The MIRA-PfAgo assay provides a reliable and practical technical tool for rapid on-site monitoring of TMUV, offering an alternative detection method that requires minimal equipment and is potentially more accessible for field applications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a new method for the rapid and sensitive detection of TMUV, which can enhance surveillance and control efforts in the waterfowl industry, particularly in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a relatively small number of clinical samples. However, the method was validated against RT-PCR, and the evidence supports a moderate confidence level in its reliability and practicality.

预先存在的针对禽 H5 甲型流感病毒的全身和鼻抗体根据童年印记而变化。

Pre-existing systemic and nasal antibodies against avian H5 influenza A viruses vary according to childhood imprinting.
作者： Peta Edler, Kevin J. Selva, Ellie Reilly, Malet Aban, Ian G. Barr, Jennifer A. Juno, Adam K. Wheatley, Amy W. Chung, Stephen J. Kent, David J. Price
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 A 病毒 (IAV) 构成持续的大流行威胁,最近的 H5 亚型 2.3.4.4b 分支具有高致病性并在动物物种和地理位置中传播。了解人类对禽类 H5 IAV 已有的免疫力可以了解人群的易感性,这是大流行防范的一个重要方面。为此,我们分析了 1928 年至 1999 年间出生、早期接触过不同 IAV 亚型的个体的 IAV HA 特异性抗体。1957 年之前出生的个体对第 1 组 HA 抗原(包括 2.3.4.4b H5 和第 1 组 HA 干抗原)的预先存在的血清抗体最高。这些出生年份特异性模式并未反映在针对 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV 或 H5 特异性记忆 B 细胞群中可检测到的有限的预先存在的血清中和抗体中。然而,它们在预先存在的 H5 鼻腔 IgG 和 IgA 滴度中很明显,这些滴度在 1957 年之前出生的个体中更高。我们的研究结果表明,早期接触所带来的免疫学偏差延伸到了鼻粘膜(IAV 复制部位)中检测到的抗体。重要性 了解对可能发生大流行的甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的现有免疫力是大流行防范的一个重要方面。这包括了解人群中预先存在的免疫力的异质性。在这里,我们证明了预先存在的 H5 IAV 抗体根据出生年份和童年印记而变化。我们证明全身抗体和鼻抗体都是如此,强调了了解流感病毒复制部位预先存在的粘膜免疫的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 A 病毒构成持续的大流行威胁,最近的 H5 亚型 2.3.4.4b 分支具有高致病性并在动物物种和地理位置中传播。了解人类对禽类 H5 IAV 已有的免疫力可以了解人群的易感性,这是大流行防范的一个重要方面。
设计与对象
为此,我们分析了 1928 年至 1999 年间出生、早期接触过不同 IAV 亚型的个体的 IAV HA 特异性抗体。1957 年之前出生的个体对第 1 组 HA 抗原(包括 2.3.4.4b H5 和第 1 组 HA 干抗原)的预先存在的血清抗体最高。
核心方法
该研究分析了早年接触过不同 IAV 亚型的个体的 HA 特异性抗体。它包括检查预先存在的 H5 鼻腔 IgG 和 IgA 滴度,1957 年之前出生的个体的滴度更高。
主要结果
这些出生年份特异性模式并未反映在针对 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV 或 H5 特异性记忆 B 细胞群中可检测到的有限的预先存在的血清中和抗体中。
解释与创新
我们的研究结果表明,早期接触所带来的免疫学偏差延伸到了鼻粘膜(IAV 复制部位)中检测到的抗体。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了了解流感病毒复制部位预先存在的粘膜免疫的重要性,这可以为大流行防范和 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
这包括了解人群中预先存在的免疫力的异质性。所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Pre-existing systemic and nasal antibodies against avian H5 influenza A viruses vary according to childhood imprinting.

Authors: Peta Edler, Kevin J. Selva, Ellie Reilly, Malet Aban, Ian G. Barr, Jennifer A. Juno, Adam K. Wheatley, Amy W. Chung, Stephen J. Kent, David J. Price
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Avian influenza A viruses (IAVs) pose a constant pandemic threat, with the recent 2.3.4.4b clade of the H5 subtype causing high pathogenicity and spreading across animal species and geographic locations. Understanding human pre-existing immunity to avian H5 IAV can inform on population susceptibility, a critical aspect of pandemic preparedness. To that end, we analyzed the IAV HA-specific antibodies across individuals born between 1928 and 1999 with different early life exposures to IAV subtypes. Individuals born prior to 1957 had the highest pre-existing serum antibodies to group 1 HA antigens, including the 2.3.4.4b H5 and a group 1 HA stem antigen. These birth year-specific patterns were not reflected in the limited pre-existing serum neutralizing antibodies detectable against a 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV or in H5-specific memory B cell populations. They were, however, evident in pre-existing nasal IgG and IgA titers to H5, which were greater in individuals born prior to 1957. Our findings demonstrate that the immunological biases afforded by early life exposure extend to antibodies detected in the nasal mucosa, the site of IAV replication. IMPORTANCE Understating pre-existing immunity to influenza A viruses (IAVs) of pandemic potential is an important aspect of pandemic preparedness. This includes an understanding of the heterogeneity of pre-existing immunity across the population. Here, we demonstrate that pre-existing antibodies to H5 IAV vary according to the year of birth and childhood imprinting. We demonstrate that this is the case for both systemic and nasal antibodies, highlighting the importance of understanding pre-existing mucosal immunity at the sites of influenza virus replication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian influenza A viruses pose a constant pandemic threat, with the recent 2.3.4.4b clade of the H5 subtype causing high pathogenicity and spreading across animal species and geographic locations. Understanding human pre-existing immunity to avian H5 IAV can inform on population susceptibility, a critical aspect of pandemic preparedness.
Design and population
To that end, we analyzed the IAV HA-specific antibodies across individuals born between 1928 and 1999 with different early life exposures to IAV subtypes. Individuals born prior to 1957 had the highest pre-existing serum antibodies to group 1 HA antigens, including the 2.3.4.4b H5 and a group 1 HA stem antigen.
Core methods
The study analyzed HA-specific antibodies across individuals with different early life exposures to IAV subtypes. It included the examination of pre-existing nasal IgG and IgA titers to H5, which were greater in individuals born prior to 1957.
Main results
These birth year-specific patterns were not reflected in the limited pre-existing serum neutralizing antibodies detectable against a 2.3.4.4b H5 IAV or in H5-specific memory B cell populations.
Interpretation and novelty
Our findings demonstrate that the immunological biases afforded by early life exposure extend to antibodies detected in the nasal mucosa, the site of IAV replication.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of understanding pre-existing mucosal immunity at the sites of influenza virus replication, which can inform pandemic preparedness and surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
This includes an understanding of the heterogeneity of pre-existing immunity across the population. The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

柠檬酸和苯扎氯铵对组合消毒剂杀病毒功效的病毒依赖性相对贡献

Virus-Dependent Relative Contributions of Citric Acid and Benzalkonium Chloride to the Virucidal Efficacy of Combination Disinfectants
作者： Sok Song, Kyu-Sik Shin, So-Hee Park, Yong Yi Joo, Cho-Yeon Lee, Hyun‐Ok Ku, Wooseog Jeong
摘要中文翻译畜牧生产中使用的化学消毒剂通常结合柠檬酸(CA)和苯扎氯铵(BZK),但这些活性成分各自对杀病毒功效的贡献仍知之甚少。本研究量化了 CA 和 BZK 对无包膜口蹄疫病毒 (FMDV) 和有包膜 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的相对贡献。使用全因子设计评估杀病毒功效,该设计包括六种 CA 浓度和六种 BZK 浓度,接触时间分别为 3 分钟和 30 分钟。通过双向方差分析和多元线性回归分析对数缩减值 (LRV),并使用 Lindeman-Merenda-Gold (LMG) 方法估计每个预测变量的相对重要性。FMDV 失活主要由 CA 决定,CA 分别占 3 分钟和 30 分钟解释方差的 99.99% 和 99.87%,而 BZK 和交互作用项的贡献最小。相比之下,BZK 是 AIV 失活的主要决定因素,分别解释了 3 分钟和 30 分钟变异的 69.79% 和 78.02%,而 CA 的贡献较小。这些发现表明,CA-BZK 组合消毒剂中的主要活性成分在所检查的 FMDV 和 AIV 模型之间存在显着差异。量化各个成分的相对贡献,为针对不同目标病毒的兽用消毒剂的合理配制和优化提供基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解柠檬酸 (CA) 和苯扎氯铵 (BZK) 对组合消毒剂杀病毒功效的相对贡献,因为它们常用于畜牧生产,但它们各自的作用仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
该研究采用全因子设计,在接触时间为 3 分钟和 30 分钟时,采用六种 CA 浓度和六种 BZK 浓度评估了杀病毒功效,重点针对非包膜口蹄疫病毒 (FMDV) 和包膜 禽流感 病毒 (AIV)。
核心方法
通过双向方差分析和多元线性回归分析对数减少值 (LRV) 来评估杀病毒功效,并使用 Lindeman-Merenda-Gold (LMG) 方法估计每个预测因子的相对重要性。
主要结果
对于 FMDV 灭活,CA 分别占 3 分钟和 30 分钟解释方差的 99.99% 和 99.87%,而 BZK 和相互作用项贡献最​​小。相比之下,BZK 是 AIV 失活的主要决定因素,分别解释了 3 分钟和 30 分钟方差的 69.79% 和 78.02%,CA 的贡献较小。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,FMDV 和 AIV 模型中 CA-BZK 组合消毒剂的主要活性成分有所不同,这凸显了根据目标病毒定制配方的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为兽用消毒剂的合理配制和优化提供了基础,特别是针对禽流感 A病毒等不同目标病毒。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,并且结果仅针对口蹄疫病毒和禽流感病毒,这可能会影响对其他病毒的普遍适用性。

Virus-Dependent Relative Contributions of Citric Acid and Benzalkonium Chloride to the Virucidal Efficacy of Combination Disinfectants

Authors: Sok Song, Kyu-Sik Shin, So-Hee Park, Yong Yi Joo, Cho-Yeon Lee, Hyun‐Ok Ku, Wooseog Jeong
Original abstract (English)
Chemical disinfectants used in livestock production commonly combine citric acid (CA) and benzalkonium chloride (BZK), yet the respective contributions of these active ingredients to virucidal efficacy remain poorly understood. This study quantified the relative contributions of CA and BZK against the non-enveloped foot-and-mouth disease virus (FMDV) and the enveloped avian influenza virus (AIV). Virucidal efficacy was evaluated using a full-factorial design comprising six CA concentrations and six BZK concentrations at contact times of 3 and 30 min. Log reduction values (LRVs) were analyzed by two-way ANOVA and multiple linear regression, and the relative importance of each predictor was estimated using the Lindeman–Merenda–Gold (LMG) method. FMDV inactivation was primarily determined by CA, which accounted for 99.99% and 99.87% of the explained variance at 3 and 30 min, respectively, whereas BZK and the interaction term contributed minimally. In contrast, BZK was the dominant determinant of AIV inactivation, explaining 69.79% and 78.02% of the variance at 3 and 30 min, respectively, while CA made a smaller contribution. These findings demonstrate that the dominant active ingredient in CA–BZK combination disinfectants differed markedly between the FMDV and AIV models examined. Quantifying the relative contribution of individual components provides a basis for the rational formulation and optimization of veterinary disinfectants for different target viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the relative contributions of citric acid (CA) and benzalkonium chloride (BZK) to the virucidal efficacy of combination disinfectants, as these are commonly used in livestock production but their individual roles remain poorly understood.
Design and population
The study evaluated virucidal efficacy using a full-factorial design with six CA concentrations and six BZK concentrations at contact times of 3 and 30 min, focusing on the non-enveloped foot-and-mouth disease virus (FMDV) and the enveloped avian influenza virus (AIV).
Core methods
Virucidal efficacy was assessed through log reduction values (LRVs) analyzed by two-way ANOVA and multiple linear regression, with the relative importance of each predictor estimated using the Lindeman–Merenda–Gold (LMG) method.
Main results
For FMDV inactivation, CA accounted for 99.99% and 99.87% of the explained variance at 3 and 30 min, respectively, while BZK and the interaction term contributed minimally. In contrast, BZK was the dominant determinant of AIV inactivation, explaining 69.79% and 78.02% of the variance at 3 and 30 min, respectively, with a smaller contribution from CA.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings show that the dominant active ingredient in CA–BZK combination disinfectants differs between the FMDV and AIV models, highlighting the need for tailored formulations based on target viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for the rational formulation and optimization of veterinary disinfectants, particularly for different target viruses such as avian influenza A viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are specific to FMDV and AIV, which may affect generalizability to other viruses.

新型脂质纳米颗粒佐剂的比较表征揭示了不同的免疫原性和反应原性特征

Comparative characterization of novel lipid nanoparticle adjuvants reveals distinct immunogenicity and reactogenicity profiles
作者： Jiin Felgner, Jenny Hernandez-Davies, Andriy Yeromin, Jui-Lin Chiang, Yibo Li, Jacob Laster, Anthony E. Gregory, Caecilia Basirin, Aarti Jain, Erwin Strahsburger
摘要中文翻译摘要 合理设计同时提高免疫原性和降低反应原性的佐剂仍然是 疫苗 蛋白开发的一个挑战。传统佐剂,如铝盐和水包油乳剂(如 MF59、AS03 和 AddaVax),可以很好地提高抗体滴度,但在调节先天免疫途径、维持长期体液免疫或有效重定向它们通常诱导的 Th2 倾斜细胞反应方面具有不同的能力。使用模式识别受体 (PRR)(包括 Toll 样受体 (TLR),如 TLR4 和 TLR9 激动剂)作为佐剂(配制在纳米乳剂中)的最新进展,凸显了联合佐剂操纵此类反应的潜力。IVAX-1 是一种由 MPLA(TLR4 激动剂)、CpG ODN 1018(TLR9 激动剂)和 AddaVax 纳米乳剂组成的组合佐剂,就是这种策略的例证。在动物模型中,会引发强烈的 Th1 偏向反应,并且与单独的佐剂成分相比,抗感染的保护作用得到增强。然而,IVAX-1 观察到短暂的体重减轻和全身炎症增强。为了检验佐剂递送平台和 TLR 激动剂影响免疫原性和反应原性之间的平衡的假设,我们开发并表征了新型佐剂制剂,其中模型 H5N1 血凝素蛋白抗原由 AddaVax 或脂质纳米颗粒配制,掺入或不掺入 MPLA 和/或 CpG。我们的目标是确定 IVAX-1 的替代品,能够共同递送抗原和 TLR 激动剂,以降低全身反应原性,而不影响免疫原性或针对 H5N1 攻击的功效。我们的研究结果表明,TLR 激动剂、佐剂递送平台和脂质成分在先天激活、适应性免疫极化和整体 疫苗 表现中发挥着核心作用。总之,这些结果为基于蛋白质的 疫苗 的下一代组合佐剂的合理设计提供了见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决设计佐剂的挑战,在蛋白质 疫苗 开发中增强免疫原性,同时降低反应原性。背景强调,传统佐剂,如铝盐和水包油乳剂,可以有效增加抗体滴度,但在调节先天免疫途径、维持长期体液免疫或重定向 Th2 倾斜的细胞反应方面具有不同的能力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及新型佐剂制剂的开发和表征,特别关注模型 H5N1 血凝素蛋白抗原。这些制剂使用 AddaVax 或脂质纳米颗粒在动物模型中进行了测试,无论是否包含 MPLA 和 CpG ODN 1018。
核心方法
研究人员通过将 TLR 激动剂(MPLA 和 CpG ODN 1018)掺入脂质纳米颗粒或 AddaVax 中,开发并表征了新型佐剂制剂。他们在动物模型中评估了这些制剂的免疫原性和反应原性。
主要结果
在动物模型中,IVAX-1(一种包含 MPLA、CpG ODN 1018 和 AddaVax 纳米乳剂的组合佐剂)与单独的佐剂成分相比,可引发强烈的 Th1 偏向反应并增强对感染的保护。然而,IVAX-1 观察到短暂的体重减轻和全身炎症增强。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为 TLR 激动剂、佐剂递送平台和脂质成分显着影响先天激活、适应性免疫极化和 疫苗 性能的证据。这项研究为基于蛋白质的 疫苗 的下一代组合佐剂的合理设计提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为基于蛋白质的 疫苗 的下一代组合佐剂的开发提供信息,特别是针对 禽流感 A 病毒的佐剂。这可能会导致改进的 疫苗 策略,具有更好的免疫原性和更低的反应原性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,结果可能无法直接转化为人类应用。证据基于对这些模型的观察,这些模型可能无法完全捕捉人类 免疫应答 的复杂性。证据强度中等。

Comparative characterization of novel lipid nanoparticle adjuvants reveals distinct immunogenicity and reactogenicity profiles

Authors: Jiin Felgner, Jenny Hernandez-Davies, Andriy Yeromin, Jui-Lin Chiang, Yibo Li, Jacob Laster, Anthony E. Gregory, Caecilia Basirin, Aarti Jain, Erwin Strahsburger
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Rational design of adjuvants that simultaneously increase immunogenicity and reduce reactogenicity remains a challenge for protein vaccine development. Conventional adjuvants, like aluminum salts, and oil-in-water emulsions (like MF59, AS03, and AddaVax), work well to increase antibody titers but have variable abilities to modulate innate immune pathways, sustain long-term humoral immunity, or effectively redirect the Th2-skewed cellular responses they typically induce. Recent advances in the use of pattern-recognition receptors (PRRs), including Toll-like receptors (TLR) such as TLR4 and TLR9 agonists as adjuvants, formulated within nanoemulsions, have highlighted the potential of combination adjuvants for manipulating such responses. IVAX-1, a combination adjuvant consisting of MPLA (TLR4 agonist), CpG ODN 1018 (TLR9 agonist), and AddaVax nanoemulsion, exemplifies this tactic. In animal models, strong Th1-biased responses are elicited, and protection against infection is enhanced compared to individual adjuvant components. However, transient weight loss and enhanced systemic inflammation are observed with IVAX-1. To test the hypothesis that adjuvant delivery platform and TLR agonists influence the balance between immunogenicity and reactogenicity, we developed and characterized novel adjuvant formulations in which the model H5N1 hemagglutinin protein antigen was formulated with AddaVax or lipid nanoparticles, with or without incorporation of MPLA and or CpG. Our objective was to identify an alternative to IVAX-1 capable of co-delivering antigen and TLR agonists to reduce systemic reactogenicity without compromising immunogenicity or efficacy against H5N1 challenge. Our findings suggest that TLR agonist, adjuvant delivery platform and lipid composition play a central role in innate activation, adaptive immune polarization, and overall vaccine performance. Together, these results provide insight into the rational design of next-generation combination adjuvants for protein-based vaccines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of designing adjuvants that enhance immunogenicity while reducing reactogenicity in protein vaccine development. The background highlights that conventional adjuvants, such as aluminum salts and oil-in-water emulsions, effectively increase antibody titers but have variable abilities to modulate innate immune pathways, sustain long-term humoral immunity, or redirect Th2-skewed cellular responses.
Design and population
The study involved the development and characterization of novel adjuvant formulations, specifically focusing on the model H5N1 hemagglutinin protein antigen. These formulations were tested in animal models using AddaVax or lipid nanoparticles, with or without the inclusion of MPLA and CpG ODN 1018.
Core methods
The researchers developed and characterized novel adjuvant formulations by incorporating TLR agonists (MPLA and CpG ODN 1018) into lipid nanoparticles or AddaVax. They evaluated the immunogenicity and reactogenicity of these formulations in animal models.
Main results
In animal models, IVAX-1, a combination adjuvant containing MPLA, CpG ODN 1018, and AddaVax nanoemulsion, elicited strong Th1-biased responses and enhanced protection against infection compared to individual adjuvant components. However, transient weight loss and enhanced systemic inflammation were observed with IVAX-1.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence that TLR agonists, adjuvant delivery platforms, and lipid compositions significantly influence innate activation, adaptive immune polarization, and vaccine performance. This study provides new insights into the rational design of next-generation combination adjuvants for protein-based vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of next-generation combination adjuvants for protein-based vaccines, particularly those targeting avian influenza A viruses. This could lead to improved vaccine strategies with better immunogenicity and reduced reactogenicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to animal models, and the results may not directly translate to human applications. The evidence is based on observations in these models, which may not fully capture the complexities of human immune responses. The evidence strength is moderate.

关于 疫苗 设计,严重的呼吸道疾病告诉我们什么:Matrix-M 的覆盖范围不断扩大

What severe respiratory disease teaches us about vaccine design: the expanding reach of Matrix-M
作者： Robert Walker
摘要中文翻译“耐久性决定了这些优先事项,因为 Matrix-M 产生的主要偏向 Th1 的反应与保护的质量和长度相关,并影响我们整个管道的抗原和佐剂设计。”疫苗 Insights 编辑 Ashling Cannon 与 Novavax 执行副总裁兼研发主管 Robert Walker 交谈,了解肺科和重症监护医学的临床背景如何塑造 Novavax 的治疗方法疫苗 免疫学。他们讨论了其技术耐受性的机制基础、Matrix-M 佐剂在传染病和肿瘤学领域的扩展用途、H5N1 大流行防范策略、早期艰难梭菌 (C. difficile) 计划,以及 Nuvaxovid 商业化向赛诺菲过渡后 Novavax 的研究重点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨偏向 Th1 的 Matrix-M 佐剂产生的免疫反应如何为严重呼吸道疾病的 疫苗 设计重点提供信息,特别是在 禽流感 A 病毒的背景下。
设计与对象
讨论围绕Novavax的临床和研究重点展开,包括针对禽流感 A病毒的疫苗的开发,重点是H5N1和其他亚型,及其在传染病和肿瘤学中的更广泛应用。
核心方法
该分析涉及与 Novavax 执行副总裁兼研发主管 Robert Walker 的对话,他们讨论了其技术耐受性的机制基础以及 Matrix-M 佐剂在不同疾病领域的扩展用途。
主要结果
结果强调了 Matrix-M 产生的 Th1 偏向反应与预防严重呼吸道疾病(包括 禽流感 A 病毒)的质量和持续时间的相关性,及其在大流行防范策略中应用的潜力。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了了解 疫苗 设计中免疫反应的重要性,特别是对于 禽流感 A 病毒,并指出 Matrix-M 佐剂在不同治疗领域扩大影响范围的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为针对 禽流感 A 病毒和其他严重呼吸道病原体开发更有效的 疫苗 提供信息,对大流行的防范和增强保护持久性的免疫策略的设计具有影响。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于讨论,不包括直接的实验结果,这限制了其强度。然而,这些见解是以公司的研究重点为基础的,并且被认为与该主题相关。

What severe respiratory disease teaches us about vaccine design: the expanding reach of Matrix-M

Authors: Robert Walker
Original abstract (English)
“Durability informs those priorities because the predominantly Th1-biased response Matrix-M generates is relevant to the quality and length of protection and shapes our antigen and adjuvant design across the pipeline.”Ashling Cannon, Editor, Vaccine Insights , speaks with Robert Walker, Executive Vice President, Head of Research & Development, Novavax, about how a clinical background in pulmonary and critical care medicine shapes Novavax’s approach to vaccine immunology. They discuss the mechanistic basis for their technology’s tolerability profile, the expanding utility of the Matrix-M adjuvant across infectious disease and oncology, H5N1 pandemic preparedness strategy, the early-stage Clostridioides difficile (C. difficile) program, and Novavax’s research priorities following the transition of Nuvaxovid commercialization to Sanofi.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how the immunological response generated by the Th1-biased Matrix-M adjuvant informs vaccine design priorities for severe respiratory diseases, particularly in the context of avian influenza A viruses.
Design and population
The discussion centered on the clinical and research priorities of Novavax, including the development of vaccines against avian influenza A viruses, with a focus on H5N1 and other subtypes, and their broader applications in infectious disease and oncology.
Core methods
The analysis involved a conversation with Robert Walker, Executive Vice President, Head of Research & Development at Novavax, where they discussed the mechanistic basis of their technology’s tolerability profile and the expanding utility of the Matrix-M adjuvant across various disease areas.
Main results
The results highlighted the relevance of the Th1-biased response generated by Matrix-M to the quality and length of protection against severe respiratory diseases, including avian influenza A viruses, and its potential for application in pandemic preparedness strategies.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the importance of understanding immunological responses in vaccine design, particularly for avian influenza A viruses, and noted the novelty of the Matrix-M adjuvant’s expanding reach across different therapeutic areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective vaccines for avian influenza A viruses and other severe respiratory pathogens, with implications for pandemic preparedness and the design of immunological strategies that enhance durability of protection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a discussion and does not include direct experimental results, which limits its strength. However, the insights are grounded in the company’s research priorities and are considered relevant to the topic.

奶牛中 H5N1 的乳腺趋向性和牛奶介导的 传播:对单一健康的影响 监测

Mammary Gland Tropism and Milk-Mediated Transmission of H5N1 in Dairy Cattle: Implications for One Health Surveillance
作者： Kehui Zhang, Yixiang Wang, Xuanrong Wang, Fang Wang, Guanlong Xu, Jie Wang, Zhaofei Wang, Yuqiang Cheng, Heng’an Wang, Yaxian Yan
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒(H5N1 HPAIV)长期在野生水禽和家禽中传播,并继续跨越物种障碍,对牲畜生产力和公共卫生构成持续威胁。2024 年美国多个州奶牛中 H5N1 HPAIV 的 暴发 代表了该病毒在反刍动物中首次被认可的大规模 传播 事件,重塑了我们对其 宿主 系列和 传播 生态的理解。本综述总结了 流行病学、病毒学特征、传播、发病机制、监测 以及奶牛相关 H5N1 控制方面的最新进展。最初的多态 暴发 由进化枝 2.3.4.4b 基因型 B3.13 主导,而随后独立引入的基因型 D1.1 表明,重复的禽类到牛 跨物种溢出 也有助于不断发展的 暴发 生态。一个决定性特征是在牛乳腺上皮细胞中有效复制,导致牛奶中病毒滴度较高,支持与牛奶相关的暴露和挤奶相关的污染作为 传播 的合理成分,尽管不同途径的相对贡献仍未解决。受感染的牛通常表现出采食量减少、产奶量显着下降、产奶量异常和轻微的全身症状,而严重的呼吸道疾病和 死亡率 并不常见。突变(例如PB2-M631L、PA-K497R)以及NP和NS1的变化可能会促进牛细胞的复制和免疫逃避,而基因型特异性的PB2适应则凸显了哺乳动物进一步适应的潜力。迄今为止报告的人类感染主要是轻微的,并且与职业暴露有关,没有证据表明存在持续的人传人 传播。实验性 疫苗 研究已开始证明牛的免疫原性,但针对乳腺感染、病毒脱落和 传播 的保护作用仍有待确定。未来的优先事项包括澄清向乳性和 传播 动态,同时加强诊断、农场生物安全、疫苗接种、评估牛 疫苗接种 策略以及综合 One Health 监测。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,特别是 H5N1,并检查了它们对乳腺的趋向性以及奶牛中牛奶介导的 传播 的潜力。核心问题是这些病毒如何影响牲畜生产力和公共卫生,并对 One Health 监测 产生影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述基于最近关于 流行病学、病毒学、传播、发病机制 和奶牛 H5N1 控制的研究和报告。它包括多态 暴发 分析和实验性 疫苗 研究,尽管证据中没有详细说明研究的具体方法和选择标准。
共识与结论
H5N1 已在牛乳腺上皮细胞中有效复制,导致牛奶中病毒滴度较高。这支持了 传播 途径中与牛奶相关的暴露和挤奶相关的污染的可能性,尽管这些途径的相对贡献仍未解决。此外,该审查强调,人类感染 H5N1 的情况主要是轻微的,并且与职业暴露有关,没有证据表明存在持续的人际 传播。
争议与缺口
关于不同 传播 路线的相对贡献,尚有未解决的证据。此外,虽然实验性 疫苗 研究已显示牛的免疫原性,但该评论指出,针对乳腺感染、病毒脱落和 传播 的保护作用尚未确定。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议未来的研究应阐明 H5N1 的乳腺倾向和 传播 动力学。它还强调需要改进诊断、增强农场生物安全以及评估牛的 疫苗接种 策略。Integrated One Health 监测 被强调为监测和控制 H5N1 传播的优先事项。

Mammary Gland Tropism and Milk-Mediated Transmission of H5N1 in Dairy Cattle: Implications for One Health Surveillance

Authors: Kehui Zhang, Yixiang Wang, Xuanrong Wang, Fang Wang, Guanlong Xu, Jie Wang, Zhaofei Wang, Yuqiang Cheng, Heng’an Wang, Yaxian Yan
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 virus (H5N1 HPAIV) has long circulated in wild waterfowl and poultry and continues to cross species barriers, posing sustained threats to livestock productivity and public health. The 2024 outbreak of H5N1 HPAIV in dairy cattle across multiple U.S. states represents the first recognized large-scale transmission event of this virus in ruminants, reshaping our understanding of its host range and transmission ecology. This review summarizes recent advances in the epidemiology, virological characteristics, transmission, pathogenesis, surveillance, and control of dairy cattle-associated H5N1. The initial multistate outbreak was dominated by clade 2.3.4.4b genotype B3.13, whereas subsequent independent introductions of genotype D1.1 demonstrated that repeated avian-to-cattle spillover also contributes to the evolving outbreak ecology. A defining feature is efficient replication in bovine mammary epithelial cells, resulting in high viral titers in milk, supporting milk-associated exposure and milking-related contamination as plausible components of transmission, although the relative contribution of different routes remains unresolved. Infected cattle typically show reduced feed intake, a marked decline in milk production, abnormal milk, and mild systemic signs, whereas severe respiratory disease and mortality are uncommon. Mutations such as PB2-M631L, PA-K497R, and changes in NP and NS1 may promote replication and immune evasion in bovine cells, while genotype-specific PB2 adaptations highlight the potential for further mammalian adaptation. Human infections reported to date have been predominantly mild and associated with occupational exposure, with no evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission. Experimental vaccine studies have begun to demonstrate immunogenicity in cattle, although protection against mammary infection, viral shedding, and transmission remains to be established. Future priorities include clarifying mammary tropism and transmission dynamics, while strengthening diagnostics, farm biosecurity, vaccination, evaluating cattle vaccination strategies, and integrated One Health surveillance.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5N1, and examines their tropism for the mammary gland and potential for milk-mediated transmission in dairy cattle. The central question is how these viruses affect livestock productivity and public health, with implications for One Health surveillance.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on recent studies and reports on the epidemiology, virology, transmission, pathogenesis, and control of H5N1 in dairy cattle. It includes a multistate outbreak analysis and experimental vaccine studies, though the specific methods and selection criteria for studies are not detailed in the evidence.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that H5N1 has efficiently replicated in bovine mammary epithelial cells, leading to high viral titers in milk. This supports the possibility of milk-associated exposure and milking-related contamination as transmission routes, although the relative contribution of these routes remains unresolved. Additionally, the review highlights that human infections with H5N1 are predominantly mild and linked to occupational exposure, with no evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission.
Controversies and gaps
There is unresolved evidence regarding the relative contribution of different transmission routes. Furthermore, while experimental vaccine studies have shown immunogenicity in cattle, the review notes that protection against mammary infection, viral shedding, and transmission has not yet been established.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests future research should clarify mammary tropism and transmission dynamics of H5N1. It also emphasizes the need for improved diagnostics, enhanced farm biosecurity, and the evaluation of vaccination strategies in cattle. Integrated One Health surveillance is highlighted as a priority for monitoring and controlling the spread of H5N1.

通过使用 高致病性禽流感 暴发 模拟探索综合建议和 禽流感 知识差距。

Exploring integrated advice and avian influenza knowledge gaps by using a Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza outbreak simulation.
作者： Femke W. Overbosch, Charlotte N. M. Waltz, Tomris Cesuroglu, Bart Blokland, Jeannette de Boer, Pearl A. Dykstra, Daniëlle R. M. Timmermans, Bas Ter Weel, Marijn de Bruin, Marion P. G. Koopmans
摘要中文翻译背景:对 COVID-19 大流行的初步评估表明,全社会应对大流行需要广泛的多学科专业知识。然而,目前尚不清楚独立的学科咨询委员会是否能够提供平衡、多视角的科学建议。目的:我们的目标是利用 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 暴发 的模拟,探索生物医学、社会和经济专家在应对大流行病方面提供综合建议的潜力。此外,我们的目的是确定与高致病性禽流感大流行防范相关的知识和专业知识的跨领域差距。方法:我们基于源自荷兰且与儿童严重疾病相关的虚构 HPAI 暴发 进行了两次桌面模拟练习(2024 年 4 月/5 月)。共有 22 名和 19 名来自生物医学、社会和经济科学领域的专家参加。参与者首先在不同的生物医学和社会团队中起草了 暴发 管理建议,然后在混合小组中进行综合审议和建议。审议和预模拟访谈的书面记录用于归纳、反思性主题分析。结果:单独的咨询流程在紧迫性、情况评估以及起草的最佳和最坏情况方面有所不同。混合小组审议暴露了纪律盲点并促进了相互理解。特别是,由于观点相互矛盾,学校关闭问题引起了广泛讨论。在(基础)结构和指南的协调中确定了跨领域的知识和技术差距。结论:我们的模拟练习表明,综合咨询方法可能在克服大流行期间相互矛盾的建议方面具有附加值。需要进一步的研究来探索这种观察到的附加价值是否延伸到综合咨询机构。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究旨在利用 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 暴发 的模拟,探索生物医学、社会和经济专家在流行病应对方面提供综合建议的潜力。它还试图确定与高致病性禽流感大流行防范相关的跨领域知识和专业知识差距。
证据基础与方法
该审查涉及 2024 年 4 月/5 月进行的两次桌面模拟练习,基于源自荷兰且与儿童严重疾病相关的虚构 HPAI 暴发。共有 22 名和 19 名来自生物医学、社会和经济科学领域的专家参加。使用归纳、反思性主题分析对审议和预模拟访谈的书面记录进行了分析。
共识与结论
模拟练习表明,综合咨询方法可能会在克服大流行期间相互冲突的建议方面提供附加值。混合小组审议暴露了纪律盲点并促进了相互理解,特别是在因观点冲突而关闭学校的问题上。在(基础)结构和指南的协调中确定了跨领域的知识和技术差距。
争议与缺口
单独的咨询流程在紧迫性、情况评估以及起草的最佳和最坏情况方面有所不同。该研究强调需要进一步研究,以确定观察到的综合咨询的附加价值是否延伸到实际的综合咨询机构。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步研究来探索观察到的综合咨询的附加价值是否延伸到综合咨询机构。研究结果表明,跨学科调整(基础设施)结构和指南对于解决大流行病防范方面的知识差距非常重要。

Exploring integrated advice and avian influenza knowledge gaps by using a Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza outbreak simulation.

Authors: Femke W. Overbosch, Charlotte N. M. Waltz, Tomris Cesuroglu, Bart Blokland, Jeannette de Boer, Pearl A. Dykstra, Daniëlle R. M. Timmermans, Bas Ter Weel, Marijn de Bruin, Marion P. G. Koopmans
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Initial evaluations of the COVID-19 pandemic suggested that broad multidisciplinary expertise is needed for an all of society pandemic response. However, it is unclear whether balanced, multi-perspective scientific advising is secured in separate disciplinary advisory committees. AIM: Our aim was to explore the potential of integrated advising among biomedical, societal and economic experts into pandemic response, using a simulation of a highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) outbreak. Additionally, we aimed to identify cross-cutting gaps in knowledge and expertise relevant for HPAI pandemic preparedness. METHODS: We conducted two table-top simulation exercises (April/May 2024), based on a fictitious HPAI outbreak originating in the Netherlands and associated with severe disease among children. In total, 22 and 19 experts participated, representing biomedical, societal and economic sciences. Participants first drafted an outbreak management advice in separate biomedical and societal teams, followed by integrated deliberations and advice in mixed groups. Written notes of the deliberations and pre-simulation interviews was used for an inductive, reflexive thematic analysis. RESULTS: Separate advisory processes differed in perceived urgency, situation assessment, and the drafted best- and worst-case scenarios. Mixed groups deliberations exposed disciplinary blind spots and fostered mutual understanding. In particular, school closure was discussed extensively due to conflicting perspectives. Cross-cutting knowledge and know-how gaps were identified in the alignment of (infra)structures and guidelines. CONCLUSIONS: Our simulation exercises suggest that an integrated advisory approach may have an added value in overcoming conflicting advice during pandemics. Further research is needed to explore whether this observed added value extends to an integrated advisory body.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to explore the potential of integrated advising among biomedical, societal, and economic experts in pandemic response, using a simulation of a highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) outbreak. It also sought to identify cross-cutting knowledge and expertise gaps relevant for HPAI pandemic preparedness.
Evidence base and method
The review involved two table-top simulation exercises conducted in April/May 2024, based on a fictitious HPAI outbreak originating in the Netherlands and associated with severe disease among children. A total of 22 and 19 experts participated, representing biomedical, societal, and economic sciences. Written notes of the deliberations and pre-simulation interviews were analyzed using an inductive, reflexive thematic analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
The simulation exercises revealed that integrated advisory approaches may offer added value in overcoming conflicting advice during pandemics. Mixed groups deliberations exposed disciplinary blind spots and fostered mutual understanding, particularly regarding school closure due to conflicting perspectives. Cross-cutting knowledge and know-how gaps were identified in the alignment of (infra)structures and guidelines.
Controversies and gaps
Separate advisory processes differed in perceived urgency, situation assessment, and the drafted best- and worst-case scenarios. The study highlights the need for further research to determine whether the observed added value of integrated advising extends to an actual integrated advisory body.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to explore whether the observed added value of integrated advising extends to an integrated advisory body. The findings suggest the importance of aligning (infra)structures and guidelines across disciplines to address knowledge gaps in pandemic preparedness.

病毒 跨物种溢出 的驱动因素:病原体传播方式的检查

Drivers of Viral Spillover: An Examination of How Pathogens Spread
作者： Elenoire Sole, Giulia Montalbano, Giuseppe Motta, Maria Maddalena Pansera, Angelina Midiri, Mariarita Iacopino, Paolo Liotta, Giuseppe Mancuso, Carmelo Biondo
摘要中文翻译由于人们对 禽流感、亨德拉病毒、猴痘病毒、尼帕病毒和蝙蝠冠状病毒等病毒威胁的日益担忧,人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出(从动物传染给人类的病毒 传播)的出现引起了全世界的关注。It is evident that these events deviate significantly from natural occurrences,being the conflation of ecological,environmental and social factors that are profoundly altering the boundaries between the animal and human domains.因此,为了了解人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出 的当代和未来驱动因素,协调一致、全面的全球“同一个健康”应对措施至关重要。本综述的目的是分析动物 储存宿主 和人类之间 传播 的普遍通路。这项检查涉及对历史病例的分析,包括 SARS 和埃博拉病毒的病例,以及最近发生的事件,例如 SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) 的全球大流行。本次综述的第二个目标是理解跨物种 传播 的基本机制。制定有效策略来减少人畜共患病毒的出现并预防未来的流行病取决于对这些机制的深入了解。该分析对于识别大流行病原体和防止人畜共患病毒传播至关重要。总之,该审查对当前防止人畜共患病毒传播的全球战略进行了全面评估,突显了我们理解上的差距和需要进一步研究的领域。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,研究其在人畜共患 跨物种溢出 中的作用以及跨物种 传播 的机制。核心问题是了解病毒从动物 储存宿主 传播到人类的驱动因素,重点是禽源感染及其亚型。
证据基础与方法
该综述对人畜共患病毒跨物种溢出的历史和近期病例进行了系统分析,包括禽流感 A病毒和其他相关病毒。它评估了导致这些病毒 传播 的生态、环境和社会因素的综合作用。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程,但概述了用于分析 传播 途径和机制的方法。
共识与结论
协调一致的全球“同一个健康”方法对于了解和减轻人畜共患病毒 跨物种溢出 的驱动因素至关重要。它强调了研究跨物种 传播 机制对于制定预防未来流行病的有效策略的重要性。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了对 禽流感 A 病毒的跨物种 传播 的具体机制的理解上的差距。它还指出了这些事件的复杂性,涉及多种相互作用的因素,以及需要进行更全面的研究来解决这些不确定性。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进一步研究以更好地了解 传播 A 病毒的 传播 动力学及其人畜共患潜力。它还意味着加强 监测 系统和实施有针对性的干预措施以降低未来 暴发 风险的重要性。

Drivers of Viral Spillover: An Examination of How Pathogens Spread

Authors: Elenoire Sole, Giulia Montalbano, Giuseppe Motta, Maria Maddalena Pansera, Angelina Midiri, Mariarita Iacopino, Paolo Liotta, Giuseppe Mancuso, Carmelo Biondo
Original abstract (English)
The occurrence of viral zoonotic spillover (the transmission of viruses from animals to humans) has attracted worldwide attention due to mounting concerns regarding viral threats such as avian influenza, Hendra, monkeypox, Nipah and bat coronaviruses. It is evident that these events deviate significantly from natural occurrences, being the conflation of ecological, environmental and social factors that are profoundly altering the boundaries between the animal and human domains. Consequently, in order to comprehend both the contemporary and prospective drivers of zoonotic viral spillover, a coordinated, comprehensive global One Health response is essential. The purpose of the present review is to analyze prevailing pathways of transmission between animal reservoirs and human populations. This examination involves the analysis of historical cases, including those of SARS and Ebola, as well as recent occurrences, such as the global pandemic of SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19). A secondary objective of this review is to comprehend the fundamental mechanisms of cross-species transmission. The development of effective strategies to mitigate the emergence of zoonotic viruses and prevent future pandemics is contingent on a robust understanding of these mechanisms. This analysis is key to identifying pandemic pathogens and preventing the spread of zoonotic viruses. In conclusion, the review provides a thorough evaluation of current global strategies to prevent the spread of zoonotic viruses, highlighting gaps in our understanding and areas for further research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, examining their role in zoonotic spillover and the mechanisms of cross-species transmission. The central question is to understand the drivers of viral spread from animal reservoirs to human populations, with an emphasis on avian-origin infections and their subtypes.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of historical and recent cases of zoonotic viral spillover, including avian influenza A viruses and other related viruses. It evaluates the confluence of ecological, environmental, and social factors that contribute to the transmission of these viruses. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or consensus processes, but it outlines the methodology used to analyze transmission pathways and mechanisms.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that a coordinated global One Health approach is essential to understand and mitigate the drivers of zoonotic viral spillover. It emphasizes the importance of studying the mechanisms of cross-species transmission to develop effective strategies for preventing future pandemics.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights gaps in understanding the specific mechanisms of cross-species transmission for avian influenza A viruses. It also notes the complexity of these events, which involve multiple interacting factors, and the need for more comprehensive research to address these uncertainties.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further research is needed to better understand the transmission dynamics of avian influenza A viruses and their zoonotic potential. It also implies the importance of strengthening surveillance systems and implementing targeted interventions to reduce the risk of future outbreaks.
